     16	class TmuxError extends Error {
     17	  constructor(code, message) {
     18	    super(message);
     19	    this.name = 'TmuxError';
     20	    this.code = code;
     21	  }
     22	}

     24	function parseRecords(stdout) {
     25	  return String(stdout || '')
     26	    .split('\n')
     27	    .filter((line) => line.length > 0)

     30	
     31	function toNumber(value) {
     32	  return Number(value);
     33	}
     34	
     35	function toBoolean(value) {
     36	  return value === '1';

     38	
     39	function errorFromTmux(error) {
     40	  const stderr = String(error && error.stderr || '').trim();
     41	  const message = stderr.toLowerCase();
     42	
     43	  if (error && error.code === 'ENOENT') {
     44	    return new TmuxError('TMUX_NOT_FOUND', '未找到 tmux，请先运行 brew install tmux');
     45	  }
     46	  if (message.includes('no server running on') || message.includes('failed to connect to server')) {
     47	    return new TmuxError('NO_SERVER', 'tmux 服务尚未启动');
     48	  }
     49	  if (message.includes("can't find session")) {
     50	    return new TmuxError('SESSION_NOT_FOUND', 'tmux 会话不存在或已关闭');
     51	  }
     52	  if (message.includes('duplicate session') || message.includes('session already exists')) {
     53	    return new TmuxError('SESSION_EXISTS', '已存在同名 tmux 会话');
     54	  }
     55	  return new TmuxError('COMMAND_FAILED', `tmux 操作失败：${stderr || 'unknown error'}`);
     56	}
     57	
     58	function validateTarget(target, pattern, label) {
     59	  if (typeof target !== 'string' || !pattern.test(target)) {
     60	    throw new Error(`无效的 tmux ${label}目标`);
     61	  }
     62	  return target;
     63	}
     64	
     65	function validateName(name) {
     66	  if (typeof name !== 'string') throw new Error('会话名称无效');
     67	  const normalized = name.trim();
     68	  if (!normalized || Array.from(normalized).length > 80 || /[.:\x00-\x1f\x7f]/.test(normalized)) {
     69	    throw new Error('会话名称无效');
     70	  }
     71	  return normalized;
     72	}
     73	
     74	class TmuxService {
     75	  constructor({ tmuxPath, execFileImpl, socketName, fsImpl } = {}) {
     76	    this.tmuxPath = tmuxPath || null;
     77	    this.execFileImpl = execFileImpl || ((file, args) => execFileAsync(file, args));
     78	    this.socketName = socketName || null;
     79	    this.fs = fsImpl || fs;
     80	  }
     81	
     82	  async resolveTmuxPath() {
     83	    if (this.tmuxPath) {
     84	      try {
     85	        this.fs.accessSync(this.tmuxPath, this.fs.constants.X_OK);
     86	        return this.tmuxPath;
     87	      } catch {
     88	        // Fall through to the standard Homebrew, system, and PATH candidates.
     89	      }
     90	    }
     91	
     92	    for (const candidate of TMUX_CANDIDATES) {
     93	      try {
     94	        this.fs.accessSync(candidate, this.fs.constants.X_OK);
     95	        return candidate;
     96	      } catch {
     97	        // Try the next standard Homebrew or system location.
     98	      }
     99	    }
     100	
     101	    try {
     102	      const { stdout } = await this.execFileImpl('/usr/bin/which', ['tmux']);
     103	      return String(stdout || '').trim() || null;
     104	    } catch {
     105	      return null;
     106	    }
     107	  }
     108	
     109	  async getStatus() {
     110	    const tmuxPath = await this.resolveTmuxPath();
     111	    if (!tmuxPath) return { installed: false, path: null, version: null };
     112	
     113	    try {
     114	      const { stdout } = await this.execFileImpl(tmuxPath, this.commandArgs(['-V']));
     115	      return { installed: true, path: tmuxPath, version: String(stdout || '').trim() || null };
     116	    } catch (error) {
     117	      throw errorFromTmux(error);
     118	    }
     119	  }
     120	
     121	  async listSessions() {
     122	    try {
     123	      const sessions = parseRecords((await this.run([
     124	        'list-sessions', '-F', '#{session_id}\t#{session_name}\t#{session_attached}\t#{session_windows}\t#{session_activity}\t#{session_path}',
     125	      ])).stdout).map(([id, name, attached, windows, activity, sessionPath]) => ({
     126	        id,
     127	        name,
     128	        attached: toBoolean(attached),
     129	        windows: toNumber(windows),
     130	        activity: toNumber(activity),
     131	        path: sessionPath,
     132	        paneCount: 0,
     133	        activeCommand: '',
     134	      }));
     135	      const byId = new Map(sessions.map((session) => [session.id, session]));
     136	      const panes = await this.run([
     137	        'list-panes', '-a', '-F', '#{session_id}\t#{pane_id}\t#{pane_active}\t#{pane_current_command}',
     138	      ]);
     139	
     140	      for (const [sessionId, , active, command] of parseRecords(panes.stdout)) {
     141	        const session = byId.get(sessionId);
     142	        if (!session) continue;
     143	        session.paneCount += 1;
     144	        if (toBoolean(active) && !session.activeCommand) session.activeCommand = command;
     145	      }
     146	      return sessions;
     147	    } catch (error) {
     148	      const normalized = error instanceof TmuxError ? error : errorFromTmux(error);
     149	      if (normalized.code === 'NO_SERVER') return [];
     150	      throw normalized;
     151	    }
     152	  }
     153	
     154	  async getSessionDetail(sessionTarget) {
     155	    validateTarget(sessionTarget, SESSION_TARGET, '会话');
     156	    const windows = parseRecords((await this.run([
     157	      'list-windows', '-t', sessionTarget, '-F', '#{window_id}\t#{window_index}\t#{window_name}\t#{window_active}\t#{window_panes}',
     158	    ])).stdout).map(([id, index, name, active, panes]) => ({
     159	      id, index: toNumber(index), name, active: toBoolean(active), panes: toNumber(panes),
     160	    }));
     161	    const panes = parseRecords((await this.run([
     162	      'list-panes', '-s', '-t', sessionTarget, '-F', '#{pane_id}\t#{window_id}\t#{window_index}\t#{pane_index}\t#{pane_active}\t#{pane_current_path}\t#{pane_current_command}\t#{pane_title}',
     163	    ])).stdout).map(([id, windowId, windowIndex, index, active, panePath, command, title]) => ({
     164	      id,
     165	      windowId,
     166	      windowIndex: toNumber(windowIndex),
     167	      index: toNumber(index),
     168	      active: toBoolean(active),
     169	      path: panePath,
     170	      command,
     171	      title,
     172	    }));
     173	    return { windows, panes };
     174	  }
     175	
     176	  async createSession({ name, cwd, startupCommand = '' } = {}) {
     177	    const validName = validateName(name);
     178	    this.validateCwd(cwd);
     179	    if (typeof startupCommand !== 'string' || startupCommand.length > 4096 || /[\x00\r\n]/.test(startupCommand)) {
     180	      throw new Error('启动命令不能包含换行或 NUL 字符');
     181	    }
     182	
     183	    const { stdout } = await this.run([
     184	      'new-session', '-d', '-P', '-F', '#{session_id}', '-s', validName, '-c', cwd,
     185	    ]);
     186	    const id = String(stdout || '').trim();
     187	    if (startupCommand) {
     188	      await this.run(['send-keys', '-t', id, '-l', '--', startupCommand]);
     189	      await this.run(['send-keys', '-t', id, 'Enter']);
     190	    }
     191	    return { id, name: validName };
     192	  }
     193	
     194	  async renameSession(sessionTarget, name) {
     195	    validateTarget(sessionTarget, SESSION_TARGET, '会话');
     196	    await this.run(['rename-session', '-t', sessionTarget, validateName(name)]);
     197	  }
     198	
     199	  async killSession(sessionTarget) {
     200	    validateTarget(sessionTarget, SESSION_TARGET, '会话');
     201	    await this.run(['kill-session', '-t', sessionTarget]);
     202	  }
     203	
     204	  async selectWindow(windowTarget) {
     205	    validateTarget(windowTarget, WINDOW_TARGET, '窗口');
     206	    await this.run(['select-window', '-t', windowTarget]);
     207	  }
     208	
     209	  async selectPane(paneTarget) {
     210	    validateTarget(paneTarget, PANE_TARGET, '面板');
     211	    await this.run(['select-pane', '-t', paneTarget]);
     212	  }
     213	
     214	  validateCwd(cwd) {
     215	    if (typeof cwd !== 'string' || cwd.length > 4096 || cwd.includes('\0') || !path.isAbsolute(cwd)) {
     216	      throw new Error('工作目录必须是绝对路径');
     217	    }
     218	    try {
     219	      if (!this.fs.statSync(cwd).isDirectory()) throw new Error('not a directory');
     220	    } catch {
     221	      throw new Error('工作目录必须是存在的目录');
     222	    }
     223	  }
     224	
     225	  commandArgs(args) {
     226	    return this.socketName ? ['-L', this.socketName, ...args] : args;
     227	  }
     228	
     229	  async run(args) {
     230	    const tmuxPath = await this.resolveTmuxPath();
     231	    if (!tmuxPath) throw new TmuxError('TMUX_NOT_FOUND', '未找到 tmux，请先运行 brew install tmux');
     232	    try {
     233	      return await this.execFileImpl(tmuxPath, this.commandArgs(args));
     234	    } catch (error) {
     235	      throw errorFromTmux(error);
     236	    }
     237	  }
     238	}
     239	
     240	module.exports = { TmuxError, TmuxService };

     66	  const item = document.createElement('div');
     67	  item.className = `toast ${type}`;
     68	  item.innerHTML = icon(type === 'error' ? 'info' : 'check', 16);
     69	  const text = document.createElement('span');

     134	function sessionMeta(session) {
     135	  const counts = `${session.windows || 0} 窗口 · ${session.paneCount || 0} 面板`;
     136	  return session.activeCommand ? `${counts} · ${session.activeCommand}` : counts;
     137	}
     138	
     139	function renderSessionList() {
     140	  const groups = filterAndGroupSessions(
     141	    state.sessions,
     142	    state.preferences.favorites,
     143	    state.query,
     144	    state.favoritesOnly,
     145	  );
     146	  refs.sessionList.replaceChildren();
     147	  const addGroup = (title, sessions) => {
     148	    if (!sessions.length) return;
     149	    const group = document.createElement('section');
     150	    group.className = 'session-group';
     151	    const heading = document.createElement('div');
     152	    heading.className = 'group-heading';
     153	    const label = document.createElement('span');
     154	    label.textContent = title;
     155	    const count = document.createElement('span');
     156	    count.textContent = String(sessions.length);
     157	    heading.append(label, count);
     158	    group.append(heading);
     159	    for (const session of sessions) group.append(createSessionRow(session));
     160	    refs.sessionList.append(group);
     161	  };
     162	  addGroup('收藏', groups.favorites);
     163	  addGroup(state.favoritesOnly ? '收藏会话' : '全部会话', groups.sessions);
     164	  if (!groups.favorites.length && !groups.sessions.length) {
     165	    const empty = document.createElement('div');
     166	    empty.className = 'sidebar-empty';
     167	    empty.textContent = state.query ? '没有匹配的会话' : state.favoritesOnly ? '还没有收藏会话' : '暂无 tmux 会话';
     168	    refs.sessionList.append(empty);
     169	  }
     170	  const attached = state.sessions.filter((session) => session.attached).length;
     171	  refs.sessionSummary.textContent = `${state.sessions.length} 个会话${attached ? ` · ${attached} 已连接` : ''}`;
     172	}
     173	
     174	function createSessionRow(session) {
     175	  const item = document.createElement('div');
     176	  item.className = `session-item ${session.id === state.selectedSessionId ? 'active' : ''}`;
     177	  const main = document.createElement('button');
     178	  main.className = 'session-main';
     179	  main.title = session.path || session.name;
     180	  const dot = document.createElement('span');
     181	  dot.className = `session-state ${session.attached ? 'attached' : ''}`;
     182	  const copy = document.createElement('span');
     183	  copy.className = 'session-copy';
     184	  const nameLine = document.createElement('span');
     185	  nameLine.className = 'session-name-line';
     186	  const name = document.createElement('span');
     187	  name.className = 'session-name';
     188	  name.textContent = session.name;
     189	  nameLine.append(name);
     190	  if (state.preferences.favorites.includes(session.name)) {
     191	    const star = document.createElement('span');
     192	    star.className = 'favorite-mark';
     193	    star.innerHTML = icon('star', 11);
     194	    nameLine.append(star);
     195	  }
     196	  const meta = document.createElement('span');
     197	  meta.className = 'session-meta';
     198	  meta.textContent = sessionMeta(session);
     199	  copy.append(nameLine, meta);
     200	  main.append(dot, copy);
     201	  main.addEventListener('click', () => selectSession(session));
     202	  const more = document.createElement('button');
     203	  more.className = 'icon-button session-more';
     204	  more.setAttribute('aria-label', `${session.name} 操作`);
     205	  more.innerHTML = icon('more', 16);
     206	  more.addEventListener('click', (event) => { event.stopPropagation(); openSessionMenu(session, more); });
     207	  item.append(main, more);
     208	  return item;
     209	}
     210	
     211	function renderWorkspace() {
     212	  const session = selectedSession();
     213	  if (session) {
     214	    refs.activeSessionName.textContent = session.name;
     215	    refs.activeSessionPath.textContent = shortenPath(session.path) || '本地 tmux 会话';
     216	    refs.attachedBadge.classList.toggle('hidden', !session.attached);
     217	    refs.terminalSurface.classList.remove('hidden');
     218	    refs.emptyState.classList.add('hidden');
     219	    refs.sessionMenuButton.classList.remove('hidden');
     220	    refs.workspaceStatus.textContent = `${session.windows || 0} 窗口 · ${session.paneCount || 0} 面板 · 纯本地连接`;
     221	  } else {
     222	    refs.activeSessionName.textContent = 'Tmux Desk';
     223	    refs.activeSessionPath.textContent = '选择一个本机会话开始工作';
     224	    refs.attachedBadge.classList.add('hidden');
     225	    refs.terminalSurface.classList.add('hidden');
     226	    refs.terminalReconnect.classList.add('hidden');
     227	    refs.sessionMenuButton.classList.add('hidden');
     228	    refs.panePickerWrap.classList.add('hidden');
     229	    refs.windowTabs.replaceChildren();
     230	    refs.emptyState.classList.remove('hidden');
     231	    if (!state.systemStatus.installed) {
     232	      refs.emptyTitle.textContent = '需要安装 tmux';
     233	      refs.emptyMessage.textContent = '未检测到本机 tmux。请在终端运行 brew install tmux，然后重新检测。';
     234	      refs.emptyNewButton.classList.add('hidden');
     235	      refs.emptyRefreshButton.textContent = '重新检测';
     236	    } else if (!state.sessions.length) {
     237	      refs.emptyTitle.textContent = '还没有 tmux 会话';
     238	      refs.emptyMessage.textContent = '创建第一个会话，应用会在所选目录中启动一个真实 tmux 终端。';
     239	      refs.emptyNewButton.classList.remove('hidden');
     240	      refs.emptyRefreshButton.textContent = '刷新列表';
     241	    } else {
     242	      refs.emptyTitle.textContent = '你的 tmux 工作台';
     243	      refs.emptyMessage.textContent = '从左侧选择一个会话，或新建会话开始工作。';
     244	      refs.emptyNewButton.classList.remove('hidden');
     245	      refs.emptyRefreshButton.textContent = '刷新列表';
     246	    }
     247	  }
     248	}
     249	
     250	function renderDetail() {
     251	  const session = selectedSession();
     252	  refs.windowTabs.replaceChildren();
     253	  if (!session) return;
     254	  for (const windowInfo of state.detail.windows) {
     255	    const button = document.createElement('button');
     256	    button.className = `window-tab ${windowInfo.active ? 'active' : ''}`;
     257	    button.setAttribute('role', 'tab');
     258	    button.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(windowInfo.active));
     259	    const index = document.createElement('span');
     260	    index.className = 'window-index';
     261	    index.textContent = String(windowInfo.index + 1);
     262	    const name = document.createElement('span');
     263	    name.className = 'window-name';
     264	    name.textContent = windowInfo.name;
     265	    const panes = document.createElement('span');
     266	    panes.className = 'pane-count';
     267	    panes.textContent = windowInfo.panes > 1 ? String(windowInfo.panes) : '';
     268	    button.append(index, name, panes);
     269	    button.addEventListener('click', async () => {
     270	      try { await api.selectWindow(windowInfo.id); await refreshDetail(); terminalView.focus(); }
     271	      catch (error) { toast(friendlyError(error), 'error'); }
     272	    });
     273	    refs.windowTabs.append(button);
     274	  }
     275	  refs.panePicker.replaceChildren(...state.detail.panes.map((pane) => {
     276	    const option = document.createElement('option');
     277	    option.value = pane.id;
     278	    option.selected = pane.active;
     279	    option.textContent = `${pane.windowIndex + 1}.${pane.index + 1} · ${pane.command || 'shell'} · ${shortenPath(pane.path)}`;
     280	    return option;
     281	  }));
     282	  refs.panePickerWrap.classList.toggle('hidden', state.detail.panes.length < 2);
     283	}
     284	
     285	async function refreshDetail() {
     286	  const session = selectedSession();
     287	  if (!session) return;
     288	  const targetId = session.id;
     289	  try {
     290	    const detail = await api.getSessionDetail(targetId);
     291	    if (state.selectedSessionId !== targetId) return;
     292	    state.detail = detail;
     293	    renderDetail();
     294	  } catch (error) {
     295	    if (state.selectedSessionId === targetId) toast(friendlyError(error), 'error');
     296	  }
     297	}
     298	
     299	async function selectSession(session) {
     300	  if (!session || session.id === state.selectedSessionId) {
     301	    terminalView?.focus();
     302	    return;
     303	  }
     304	  const token = ++selectionToken;
     305	  state.selectedSessionId = session.id;
     306	  state.detail = { windows: [], panes: [] };
     307	  refs.terminalReconnect.classList.add('hidden');
     308	  renderSessionList();
     309	  renderWorkspace();
     310	  renderDetail();
     311	  try {
     312	    state.preferences = await api.savePreferences({ lastSession: session.name });
     313	    await Promise.all([refreshDetail(), terminalView.attach(session.id)]);
     314	    if (token !== selectionToken) return;
     315	    refs.workspaceStatus.textContent = `${session.windows || 0} 窗口 · ${session.paneCount || 0} 面板 · 已连接`;
     316	  } catch (error) {
     317	    if (token === selectionToken) {
     318	      refs.terminalReconnect.classList.remove('hidden');
     319	      toast(friendlyError(error), 'error');
     320	    }
     321	  }
     322	}
     323	
     324	async function refreshSessions({ initial = false, notify = false } = {}) {
     325	  if (state.refreshing) return;
     326	  state.refreshing = true;
     327	  refs.refreshButton.classList.add('spinning');
     328	  try {
     329	    if (initial || !state.systemStatus.installed) state.systemStatus = await api.getSystemStatus();
     330	    const sessions = state.systemStatus.installed ? await api.listSessions() : [];
     331	    const previousId = state.selectedSessionId;
     332	    state.sessions = sessions;
     333	    if (previousId && !sessions.some((session) => session.id === previousId)) {
     334	      selectionToken += 1;
     335	      state.selectedSessionId = null;
     336	      state.detail = { windows: [], panes: [] };
     337	      await terminalView.detach();
     338	      if (!initial) toast('当前 tmux 会话已在外部关闭', 'error');
     339	    }
     340	    renderSystemStatus();
     341	    renderSessionList();
     342	    renderWorkspace();
     343	    if (state.selectedSessionId) await refreshDetail();
     344	    else if (initial && sessions.length) {
     345	      const preferred = sessions.find((session) => session.name === state.preferences.lastSession) || sessions[0];
     346	      await selectSession(preferred);
     347	    }
     348	    if (notify) toast('会话列表已刷新');
     349	  } catch (error) {
     350	    toast(friendlyError(error), 'error');
     351	  } finally {
     352	    state.refreshing = false;
     353	    refs.refreshButton.classList.remove('spinning');
     354	  }
     355	}
     356	
     357	function menuButton({ iconName, title, action, danger = false, checked = false }) {
     358	  const button = document.createElement('button');
     359	  button.className = `menu-item ${danger ? 'danger' : ''}`;
     360	  button.innerHTML = icon(iconName, 15);
     361	  const label = document.createElement('span');
     362	  label.textContent = title;
     363	  button.append(label);
     364	  if (checked) {
     365	    const check = document.createElement('span');
     366	    check.className = 'menu-check';
     367	    check.innerHTML = icon('check', 14);
     368	    button.append(check);
     369	  }
     370	  button.addEventListener('click', action);
     371	  return button;
     372	}
     373	
     374	function separator() {
     375	  const item = document.createElement('div');
     376	  item.className = 'menu-separator';
     377	  return item;
     378	}
     379	
     380	function placePopover(popover, anchor) {
     381	  popover.classList.remove('hidden');
     382	  const rect = anchor.getBoundingClientRect();
     383	  const width = popover.offsetWidth;
     384	  const height = popover.offsetHeight;
     385	  popover.style.left = `${Math.min(window.innerWidth - width - 8, Math.max(8, rect.right - width))}px`;
     386	  popover.style.top = `${Math.min(window.innerHeight - height - 8, rect.bottom + 5)}px`;
     387	}
     388	
     389	function closeFloatingMenus() {
     390	  refs.contextMenu.classList.add('hidden');
     391	  refs.themePopover.classList.add('hidden');
     392	}
     393	
     394	function openSessionMenu(session, anchor) {
     395	  closeFloatingMenus();
     396	  const isFavorite = state.preferences.favorites.includes(session.name);
     397	  refs.contextMenu.replaceChildren(
     398	    menuButton({ iconName: 'terminal', title: '连接会话', action: () => { closeFloatingMenus(); selectSession(session); } }),
     399	    menuButton({ iconName: 'star', title: isFavorite ? '取消收藏' : '收藏会话', action: async () => {
     400	      closeFloatingMenus();
     401	      try { state.preferences = await api.toggleFavorite(session.name); renderSessionList(); }
     402	      catch (error) { toast(friendlyError(error), 'error'); }
     403	    } }),
     404	    separator(),
     405	    menuButton({ iconName: 'edit', title: '重命名', action: () => { closeFloatingMenus(); openRenameModal(session); } }),
     406	    menuButton({ iconName: 'copy', title: '复制名称', action: async () => {
     407	      closeFloatingMenus();
     408	      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(session.name);
     409	      toast('会话名称已复制');
     410	    } }),
     411	    separator(),
     412	    menuButton({ iconName: 'trash', title: '关闭会话…', danger: true, action: () => { closeFloatingMenus(); openKillModal(session); } }),
     413	  );
     414	  placePopover(refs.contextMenu, anchor);
     415	}
     416	
     417	function openModal(modal, focusTarget) {
     418	  closeFloatingMenus();
     419	  modal.classList.remove('hidden');
     420	  requestAnimationFrame(() => focusTarget?.focus());
     421	}
     422	
     423	function closeModal(modal) { modal.classList.add('hidden'); }
     424	
     425	function openNewSessionModal() {
     426	  refs.sessionForm.reset();
     427	  const session = selectedSession();
     428	  refs.sessionCwdInput.value = session?.path || '';
     429	  openModal(refs.sessionModal, refs.sessionNameInput);
     430	}
     431	
     432	function openRenameModal(session = selectedSession()) {
     433	  if (!session) return;
     434	  refs.renameModal.dataset.sessionId = session.id;
     435	  refs.renameModal.dataset.oldName = session.name;
     436	  refs.renameInput.value = session.name;
     437	  openModal(refs.renameModal, refs.renameInput);
     438	  refs.renameInput.select();
     439	}
     440	
     441	function openKillModal(session = selectedSession()) {
     442	  if (!session) return;
     443	  refs.killModal.dataset.sessionId = session.id;
     444	  refs.killMessage.textContent = `“${session.name}” 中的 ${session.paneCount || 0} 个面板及其进程都会被终止。此操作不可撤销。`;
     445	  openModal(refs.killModal, refs.confirmKillButton);
     446	}
     447	
     448	function openSettings() {
     449	  renderThemeControls();
     450	  openModal(refs.settingsModal, refs.settingsModal.querySelector('.theme-card'));
     451	}
     452	
     453	function commands() {
     454	  const session = selectedSession();
     455	  return [
     456	    { id: 'new', title: '新建会话', subtitle: '创建一个本地 tmux 会话', shortcut: '⌘N', icon: 'plus', action: openNewSessionModal },
     457	    { id: 'refresh', title: '刷新会话', subtitle: '重新读取本机 tmux 状态', shortcut: '⌘R', icon: 'refresh', action: () => refreshSessions({ notify: true }) },
     458	    { id: 'search', title: '搜索会话', subtitle: '聚焦左侧搜索框', shortcut: '⌘F', icon: 'search', action: () => refs.sessionSearch.focus() },
     459	    { id: 'favorites', title: state.favoritesOnly ? '显示全部会话' : '只看收藏', subtitle: '切换侧栏筛选', icon: 'star', action: () => setFavoritesOnly(!state.favoritesOnly) },
     460	    { id: 'theme', title: '切换主题', subtitle: '亮色、暗色、跟随系统', shortcut: '⌘⇧T', keywords: ['light', 'dark'], icon: 'palette', action: cycleTheme },
     461	    { id: 'settings', title: '打开设置', subtitle: '外观与本地环境信息', icon: 'settings', action: openSettings },
     462	    ...(session ? [
     463	      { id: 'rename', title: `重命名 “${session.name}”`, subtitle: '修改 tmux 会话名称', icon: 'edit', action: () => openRenameModal(session) },
     464	      { id: 'favorite', title: state.preferences.favorites.includes(session.name) ? '取消收藏当前会话' : '收藏当前会话', subtitle: session.name, icon: 'star', action: () => toggleSelectedFavorite() },
     465	      { id: 'kill', title: `关闭 “${session.name}”…`, subtitle: '终止此会话中的所有进程', icon: 'trash', action: () => openKillModal(session) },
     466	    ] : []),
     467	  ];
     468	}
     469	
     470	function openCommandPalette() {
     471	  refs.commandInput.value = '';
     472	  state.commandIndex = 0;
     473	  refs.commandPalette.classList.remove('hidden');
     474	  renderCommandResults();
     475	  requestAnimationFrame(() => refs.commandInput.focus());
     476	}
     477	
     478	function closeCommandPalette() { refs.commandPalette.classList.add('hidden'); }
     479	
     480	function filteredCommands() { return searchCommands(commands(), refs.commandInput.value); }
     481	
     482	function renderCommandResults() {
     483	  const items = filteredCommands();
     484	  if (state.commandIndex >= items.length) state.commandIndex = Math.max(0, items.length - 1);
     485	  refs.commandResults.replaceChildren();
     486	  if (!items.length) {
     487	    const empty = document.createElement('div');
     488	    empty.className = 'command-empty';
     489	    empty.textContent = '没有匹配的命令';
     490	    refs.commandResults.append(empty);
     491	    return;
     492	  }
     493	  items.forEach((command, index) => {
     494	    const button = document.createElement('button');
     495	    button.className = `command-result ${index === state.commandIndex ? 'selected' : ''}`;
     496	    button.setAttribute('role', 'option');
     497	    button.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(index === state.commandIndex));
     498	    const iconBox = document.createElement('span');
     499	    iconBox.className = 'command-result-icon';
     500	    iconBox.innerHTML = icon(command.icon || 'command', 16);
     501	    const copy = document.createElement('span');
     502	    copy.className = 'command-result-copy';
     503	    const title = document.createElement('span');
     504	    title.className = 'command-result-title';
     505	    title.textContent = command.title;
     506	    const subtitle = document.createElement('span');
     507	    subtitle.className = 'command-result-subtitle';
     508	    subtitle.textContent = command.subtitle || '';
     509	    copy.append(title, subtitle);
     510	    button.append(iconBox, copy);
     511	    if (command.shortcut) {
     512	      const shortcut = document.createElement('kbd');
     513	      shortcut.textContent = command.shortcut;
     514	      button.append(shortcut);
     515	    }
     516	    button.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
     517	      state.commandIndex = index;
     518	      [...refs.commandResults.querySelectorAll('.command-result')].forEach((item, itemIndex) => {
     519	        item.classList.toggle('selected', itemIndex === index);
     520	        item.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(itemIndex === index));
     521	      });
     522	    });
     523	    button.addEventListener('click', () => executeCommand(command));
     524	    refs.commandResults.append(button);
     525	  });
     526	  refs.commandResults.querySelector('.selected')?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' });
     527	}
     528	
     529	function executeCommand(command = filteredCommands()[state.commandIndex]) {
     530	  if (!command) return;
     531	  closeCommandPalette();
     532	  Promise.resolve(command.action()).catch((error) => toast(friendlyError(error), 'error'));
     533	}
     534	
     535	function setFavoritesOnly(value) {
     536	  state.favoritesOnly = Boolean(value);
     537	  document.querySelectorAll('[data-view]').forEach((button) => {
     538	    button.classList.toggle('active', button.dataset.view === (state.favoritesOnly ? 'favorites' : 'workspace'));
     539	  });
     540	  renderSessionList();
     541	}
     542	
     543	function cycleTheme() {
     544	  const modes = ['light', 'dark', 'system'];
     545	  setThemePreference(modes[(modes.indexOf(state.preferences.theme) + 1) % modes.length]);
     546	}
     547	
     548	async function toggleSelectedFavorite() {
     549	  const session = selectedSession();
     550	  if (!session) return;
     551	  state.preferences = await api.toggleFavorite(session.name);
     552	  renderSessionList();
     553	}
     554	
     555	function bindEvents() {
     556	  refs.refreshButton.addEventListener('click', () => refreshSessions({ notify: true }));
     557	  refs.emptyRefreshButton.addEventListener('click', () => refreshSessions({ initial: !state.systemStatus.installed, notify: true }));
     558	  refs.newSessionButton.addEventListener('click', openNewSessionModal);
     559	  refs.emptyNewButton.addEventListener('click', openNewSessionModal);
     560	  refs.sessionSearch.addEventListener('input', () => { state.query = refs.sessionSearch.value; renderSessionList(); });
     561	  document.querySelectorAll('[data-view]').forEach((button) => button.addEventListener('click', () => setFavoritesOnly(button.dataset.view === 'favorites')));
     562	  refs.settingsButton.addEventListener('click', openSettings);
     563	  refs.commandHint.addEventListener('click', openCommandPalette);
     564	  refs.themeButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
     565	    if (!refs.themePopover.classList.contains('hidden')) return closeFloatingMenus();
     566	    closeFloatingMenus();
     567	    renderThemeControls();
     568	    placePopover(refs.themePopover, refs.themeButton);
     569	  });
     570	  refs.sessionMenuButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
     571	    const session = selectedSession();
     572	    if (session) openSessionMenu(session, refs.sessionMenuButton);
     573	  });
     574	  refs.panePicker.addEventListener('change', async () => {
     575	    try { await api.selectPane(refs.panePicker.value); await refreshDetail(); terminalView.focus(); }
     576	    catch (error) { toast(friendlyError(error), 'error'); }
     577	  });
     578	  refs.reconnectButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
     579	    const session = selectedSession();
     580	    if (!session) return;
     581	    refs.terminalReconnect.classList.add('hidden');
     582	    terminalView.attach(session.id)
     583	      .then(() => { refs.workspaceStatus.textContent = '已重新连接本地 tmux 会话'; })
     584	      .catch((error) => { refs.terminalReconnect.classList.remove('hidden'); toast(friendlyError(error), 'error'); });
     585	  });
     586	
     587	  refs.chooseDirectoryButton.addEventListener('click', async () => {
     588	    try {
     589	      const directory = await api.chooseDirectory();
     590	      if (directory) refs.sessionCwdInput.value = directory;
     591	    } catch (error) { toast(friendlyError(error), 'error'); }
     592	  });
     593	  refs.sessionForm.addEventListener('submit', async (event) => {
     594	    event.preventDefault();
     595	    const submit = refs.sessionForm.querySelector('[type="submit"]');
     596	    submit.disabled = true;
     597	    try {
     598	      const created = await api.createSession({
     599	        name: refs.sessionNameInput.value,
     600	        cwd: refs.sessionCwdInput.value,
     601	        startupCommand: refs.startupCommandInput.value,
     602	      });
     603	      closeModal(refs.sessionModal);
     604	      await refreshSessions();
     605	      const session = state.sessions.find((item) => item.id === created.id || item.name === created.name);
     606	      if (session) await selectSession(session);
     607	      toast(`已创建会话 ${created.name}`);
     608	    } catch (error) { toast(friendlyError(error), 'error'); }
     609	    finally { submit.disabled = false; }
     610	  });
     611	  refs.renameForm.addEventListener('submit', async (event) => {
     612	    event.preventDefault();
     613	    try {
     614	      await api.renameSession({
     615	        target: refs.renameModal.dataset.sessionId,
     616	        oldName: refs.renameModal.dataset.oldName,
     617	        name: refs.renameInput.value,
     618	      });
     619	      state.preferences = await api.getPreferences();
     620	      closeModal(refs.renameModal);
     621	      await refreshSessions();
     622	      toast('会话已重命名');
     623	    } catch (error) { toast(friendlyError(error), 'error'); }
     624	  });
     625	  refs.confirmKillButton.addEventListener('click', async () => {
     626	    const target = refs.killModal.dataset.sessionId;
     627	    refs.confirmKillButton.disabled = true;
     628	    try {
     629	      if (target === state.selectedSessionId) await terminalView.detach();
     630	      await api.killSession(target);
     631	      if (target === state.selectedSessionId) state.selectedSessionId = null;
     632	      closeModal(refs.killModal);
     633	      await refreshSessions();
     634	      toast('tmux 会话已关闭');
     635	    } catch (error) { toast(friendlyError(error), 'error'); }
     636	    finally { refs.confirmKillButton.disabled = false; }
     637	  });
     638	  document.querySelectorAll('.modal-cancel').forEach((button) => button.addEventListener('click', () => closeModal(button.closest('.modal-backdrop'))));
     639	  document.querySelectorAll('.modal-close').forEach((button) => button.addEventListener('click', () => closeModal(button.closest('.modal-backdrop'))));
     640	  document.querySelectorAll('.modal-backdrop').forEach((backdrop) => backdrop.addEventListener('mousedown', (event) => {
     641	    if (event.target === backdrop) closeModal(backdrop);
     642	  }));
     643	
     644	  refs.commandInput.addEventListener('input', () => { state.commandIndex = 0; renderCommandResults(); });
     645	  refs.commandPalette.addEventListener('mousedown', (event) => { if (event.target === refs.commandPalette) closeCommandPalette(); });
     646	  document.addEventListener('mousedown', (event) => {
     647	    if (!refs.contextMenu.contains(event.target) && !refs.themePopover.contains(event.target)) closeFloatingMenus();
     648	  });
     649	  window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyboard);
     650	  systemTheme.addEventListener('change', () => { if (state.preferences.theme === 'system') applyCurrentTheme(); });
     651	  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { if (!document.hidden) refreshSessions(); });
     652	  api.onAppCommand((command) => handleAppCommand(command));
     653	  window.addEventListener('beforeunload', () => {
     654	    clearInterval(pollTimer);
     655	    terminalView?.dispose();
     656	  });
     657	}
     658	
     659	function handleKeyboard(event) {
     660	  if (!refs.commandPalette.classList.contains('hidden')) {
     661	    const items = filteredCommands();
     662	    if (event.key === 'ArrowDown' && items.length) { event.preventDefault(); state.commandIndex = Math.min(items.length - 1, state.commandIndex + 1); renderCommandResults(); }
     663	    else if (event.key === 'ArrowUp') { event.preventDefault(); state.commandIndex = Math.max(0, state.commandIndex - 1); renderCommandResults(); }
     664	    else if (event.key === 'Enter') { event.preventDefault(); executeCommand(); }
     665	    else if (event.key === 'Escape') { event.preventDefault(); closeCommandPalette(); }
     666	    return;
     667	  }
     668	  const meta = event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey;
     669	  if (meta && !event.altKey && event.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') { event.preventDefault(); openCommandPalette(); }
     670	  else if (meta && !event.altKey && event.key.toLowerCase() === 'n') { event.preventDefault(); openNewSessionModal(); }
     671	  else if (meta && !event.altKey && event.key.toLowerCase() === 'f') { event.preventDefault(); refs.sessionSearch.focus(); refs.sessionSearch.select(); }
     672	  else if (meta && !event.altKey && event.key.toLowerCase() === 'r') { event.preventDefault(); refreshSessions({ notify: true }); }
     673	  else if (meta && event.shiftKey && event.key.toLowerCase() === 't') { event.preventDefault(); cycleTheme(); }
     674	  else if (meta && /^[1-9]$/.test(event.key)) {
     675	    const windowInfo = state.detail.windows[Number(event.key) - 1];
     676	    if (windowInfo) { event.preventDefault(); api.selectWindow(windowInfo.id).then(refreshDetail).catch((error) => toast(friendlyError(error), 'error')); }
     677	  } else if (event.key === 'Escape') {
     678	    closeFloatingMenus();
     679	    const openModalElement = [...document.querySelectorAll('.modal-backdrop:not(.hidden)')].pop();
     680	    if (openModalElement) closeModal(openModalElement);
     681	  }
     682	}
     683	
     684	function handleAppCommand(command) {
     685	  if (command === 'new-session') openNewSessionModal();
     686	  else if (command === 'command-palette') openCommandPalette();
     687	  else if (command === 'refresh') refreshSessions({ notify: true });
     688	  else if (command === 'search') refs.sessionSearch.focus();
     689	}
     690	
     691	async function initialize() {
     692	  cacheElements();
     693	  installIcons();
     694	  if (!api) {
     695	    refs.emptyTitle.textContent = '无法连接本地服务';
     696	    refs.emptyMessage.textContent = '请通过 Tmux Desk 桌面应用打开此界面。';
     697	    refs.emptyActions.classList.add('hidden');
     698	    return;
     699	  }
     700	  terminalView = new TerminalView({
     701	    container: refs.terminalMount,
     702	    api,
     703	    onExit: () => {
     704	      refs.terminalReconnect.classList.remove('hidden');
     705	      refs.workspaceStatus.textContent = '连接已结束 · tmux 会话可能仍在运行';
     706	    },
     707	    onError: (error) => toast(friendlyError(error), 'error'),
     708	  });
     709	  bindEvents();
     710	  try {
     711	    state.preferences = await api.getPreferences();
     712	    applyCurrentTheme();
     713	    await refreshSessions({ initial: true });
     714	    pollTimer = setInterval(() => { if (!document.hidden) refreshSessions(); }, 2000);
     715	  } catch (error) {
     716	    toast(friendlyError(error), 'error');
     717	    renderWorkspace();
     718	  }
     719	}
     720	
     721	initialize();
     722

  ⎿  1	const os = require('node:os');
     2	
     3	const SESSION_TARGET = /^\$\d+$/;
     4	const MAX_INPUT_LENGTH = 1024 * 1024;
     5	
     6	function validateDimensions(cols, rows) {
     7	  if (!Number.isInteger(cols) || !Number.isInteger(rows) || cols < 2 || cols > 1000 || rows < 1 || rows > 500) {
     8	    throw new Error('终端尺寸无效');
     9	  }
     10	}
     11	
     12	class TerminalManager {
     13	  constructor({ ptyModule, sendEvent, tmuxPath = 'tmux', socketName, env } = {}) {
     14	    this.pty = ptyModule || require('node-pty');
     15	    this.sendEvent = typeof sendEvent === 'function' ? sendEvent : () => {};
     16	    this.tmuxPath = tmuxPath;
     17	    this.socketName = socketName || null;
     18	    this.env = { ...(env || process.env) };
     19	    this.active = null;
     20	    this.nextTerminalId = 1;
     21	  }
     22	
     23	  attach({ sessionId, cols, rows } = {}) {
     24	    if (typeof sessionId !== 'string' || !SESSION_TARGET.test(sessionId)) {
     25	      throw new Error('tmux 会话目标无效');
     26	    }
     27	    validateDimensions(cols, rows);
     28	    this.disposeActive();
     29	
     30	    const terminalId = this.nextTerminalId++;
     31	    const args = [
     32	      ...(this.socketName ? ['-L', this.socketName] : []),
     33	      'attach-session', '-t', sessionId,
     34	    ];
     35	    const process = this.pty.spawn(this.tmuxPath, args, {
     36	      name: 'xterm-256color',
     37	      cols,
     38	      rows,
     39	      cwd: this.env.HOME || os.homedir(),
     40	      env: { ...this.env, TERM: 'xterm-256color', COLORTERM: 'truecolor' },
     41	    });
     42	    const active = { terminalId, process, disposables: [] };
     43	    active.disposables.push(process.onData((data) => {
     44	      this.sendEvent('terminal:data', { terminalId, data });
     45	    }));
     46	    active.disposables.push(process.onExit(({ exitCode, signal } = {}) => {
     47	      this.sendEvent('terminal:exit', {
     48	        terminalId,
     49	        exitCode: Number.isInteger(exitCode) ? exitCode : null,
     50	        signal: Number.isInteger(signal) ? signal : null,
     51	      });
     52	      if (this.active?.terminalId === terminalId) {
     53	        this.disposeListeners(active);
     54	        this.active = null;
     55	      }
     56	    }));
     57	    this.active = active;
     58	    return { terminalId };
     59	  }
     60	
     61	  write({ terminalId, data } = {}) {
     62	    if (typeof data !== 'string' || data.length > MAX_INPUT_LENGTH) {
     63	      throw new Error('终端输入无效');
     64	    }
     65	    if (!this.isActive(terminalId)) return false;
     66	    this.active.process.write(data);
     67	    return true;
     68	  }
     69	
     70	  resize({ terminalId, cols, rows } = {}) {
     71	    validateDimensions(cols, rows);
     72	    if (!this.isActive(terminalId)) return false;
     73	    this.active.process.resize(cols, rows);
     74	    return true;
     75	  }
     76	
     77	  isActive(terminalId) {
     78	    return Number.isInteger(terminalId) && this.active?.terminalId === terminalId;
     79	  }
     80	
     81	  disposeListeners(active) {
     82	    for (const disposable of active.disposables.splice(0)) {
     83	      try { disposable?.dispose?.(); } catch {}
     84	    }
     85	  }
     86	
     87	  disposeActive() {
     88	    if (!this.active) return;
     89	    const active = this.active;
     90	    this.active = null;
     91	    this.disposeListeners(active);
     92	    try { active.process.kill(); } catch {}
     93	  }
     94	
     95	  dispose() {
     96	    this.disposeActive();
     97	  }
     98	}
     99	
     100	module.exports = { TerminalManager, validateDimensions };
     101

  ⎿  1	const path = require('node:path');
     2	
     3	const TARGET_PATTERNS = {
     4	  session: /^\$\d+$/,
     5	  window: /^@\d+$/,
     6	  pane: /^%\d+$/,
     7	};
     8	const VALID_THEMES = new Set(['light', 'dark', 'system']);
     9	const IPC_CHANNELS = Object.freeze([
     10	  'system:get-status',
     11	  'system:choose-directory',
     12	  'sessions:list',
     13	  'sessions:detail',
     14	  'sessions:create',
     15	  'sessions:rename',
     16	  'sessions:kill',
     17	  'sessions:toggle-favorite',
     18	  'windows:select',
     19	  'panes:select',
     20	  'preferences:get',
     21	  'preferences:save',
     22	  'terminal:attach',
     23	  'terminal:write',
     24	  'terminal:resize',
     25	  'terminal:detach',
     26	]);
     27	
     28	function validateTarget(value, kind = 'session') {
     29	  const pattern = TARGET_PATTERNS[kind];
     30	  if (!pattern || typeof value !== 'string' || !pattern.test(value)) {
     31	    throw new Error('tmux 目标无效');
     32	  }
     33	  return value;
     34	}
     35	
     36	function validateSessionName(value) {
     37	  if (typeof value !== 'string') throw new Error('会话名称无效');
     38	  const name = value.trim();
     39	  if (!name || Array.from(name).length > 80 || /[.:\x00-\x1f\x7f]/.test(name)) {
     40	    throw new Error('会话名称无效');
     41	  }
     42	  return name;
     43	}
     44	
     45	function validateSessionInput(value) {
     46	  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error('会话参数无效');
     47	  const name = validateSessionName(value.name);
     48	  if (typeof value.cwd !== 'string' || value.cwd.length > 4096 || value.cwd.includes('\0') || !path.isAbsolute(value.cwd)) {
     49	    throw new Error('工作目录无效');
     50	  }
     51	  const startupCommand = value.startupCommand ?? '';
     52	  if (typeof startupCommand !== 'string' || startupCommand.length > 4096 || /[\x00\r\n]/.test(startupCommand)) {
     53	    throw new Error('启动命令无效');
     54	  }
     55	  return { name, cwd: value.cwd, startupCommand };
     56	}
     57	
     58	function validateTerminalId(value) {
     59	  if (!Number.isInteger(value) || value < 1 || value > Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) {
     60	    throw new Error('终端编号无效');
     61	  }
     62	  return value;
     63	}
     64	
     65	function validateTerminalSize(cols, rows) {
     66	  if (!Number.isInteger(cols) || !Number.isInteger(rows) || cols < 2 || cols > 1000 || rows < 1 || rows > 500) {
     67	    throw new Error('终端尺寸无效');
     68	  }
     69	  return { cols, rows };
     70	}
     71	
     72	function validateTerminalPayload(kind, value) {
     73	  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error('终端参数无效');
     74	  if (kind === 'attach') {
     75	    return { sessionId: validateTarget(value.sessionId), ...validateTerminalSize(value.cols, value.rows) };
     76	  }
     77	  const terminalId = validateTerminalId(value.terminalId);
     78	  if (kind === 'write') {
     79	    if (typeof value.data !== 'string' || value.data.length > 1024 * 1024) throw new Error('终端输入无效');
     80	    return { terminalId, data: value.data };
     81	  }
     82	  if (kind === 'resize') return { terminalId, ...validateTerminalSize(value.cols, value.rows) };
     83	  throw new Error('终端操作无效');
     84	}
     85	
     86	function validateWindowBounds(value) {
     87	  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || !['x', 'y', 'width', 'height'].every((key) => Number.isFinite(value[key]))) {
     88	    throw new Error('窗口尺寸无效');
     89	  }
     90	  if (value.width < 640 || value.height < 480) throw new Error('窗口尺寸无效');
     91	  return Object.fromEntries(['x', 'y', 'width', 'height'].map((key) => [key, Math.round(value[key])]));
     92	}
     93	
     94	function validatePreferencesPatch(value) {
     95	  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error('偏好设置无效');
     96	  const allowed = new Set(['theme', 'lastSession', 'windowBounds']);
     97	  for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
     98	    if (!allowed.has(key)) throw new Error('偏好字段无效');
     99	  }
     100	  const result = {};
     101	  if ('theme' in value) {
     102	    if (!VALID_THEMES.has(value.theme)) throw new Error('主题无效');
     103	    result.theme = value.theme;
     104	  }
     105	  if ('lastSession' in value) {
     106	    if (value.lastSession === null) result.lastSession = null;
     107	    else result.lastSession = validateSessionName(value.lastSession);
     108	  }
     109	  if ('windowBounds' in value) result.windowBounds = validateWindowBounds(value.windowBounds);
     110	  return result;
     111	}
     112	
     113	function registerIpc({ ipcMain, tmuxService, terminalManager, preferencesStore, chooseDirectory }) {
     114	  const handlers = {
     115	    'system:get-status': () => tmuxService.getStatus(),
     116	    'system:choose-directory': () => chooseDirectory(),
     117	    'sessions:list': () => tmuxService.listSessions(),
     118	    'sessions:detail': (_event, target) => tmuxService.getSessionDetail(validateTarget(target)),
     119	    'sessions:create': (_event, input) => tmuxService.createSession(validateSessionInput(input)),
     120	    'sessions:rename': async (_event, input) => {
     121	      if (!input || typeof input !== 'object') throw new Error('重命名参数无效');
     122	      const target = validateTarget(input.target);
     123	      const oldName = validateSessionName(input.oldName);
     124	      const name = validateSessionName(input.name);
     125	      await tmuxService.renameSession(target, name);
     126	      await preferencesStore.renameFavorite(oldName, name);
     127	      return { name };
     128	    },
     129	    'sessions:kill': (_event, target) => tmuxService.killSession(validateTarget(target)),
     130	    'sessions:toggle-favorite': (_event, name) => preferencesStore.toggleFavorite(validateSessionName(name)),
     131	    'windows:select': (_event, target) => tmuxService.selectWindow(validateTarget(target, 'window')),
     132	    'panes:select': (_event, target) => tmuxService.selectPane(validateTarget(target, 'pane')),
     133	    'preferences:get': () => preferencesStore.load(),
     134	    'preferences:save': (_event, patch) => preferencesStore.save(validatePreferencesPatch(patch)),
     135	    'terminal:attach': (_event, payload) => terminalManager.attach(validateTerminalPayload('attach', payload)),
     136	    'terminal:write': (_event, payload) => terminalManager.write(validateTerminalPayload('write', payload)),
     137	    'terminal:resize': (_event, payload) => terminalManager.resize(validateTerminalPayload('resize', payload)),
     138	    'terminal:detach': () => { terminalManager.dispose(); return true; },
     139	  };
     140	  for (const channel of IPC_CHANNELS) ipcMain.handle(channel, handlers[channel]);
     141	  return handlers;
     142	}
     143	
     144	module.exports = {
     145	  IPC_CHANNELS,
     146	  registerIpc,
     147	  validatePreferencesPatch,
     148	  validateSessionInput,
     149	  validateTarget,
     150	  validateTerminalPayload,
     151	};
     152

  ⎿  1	const { app, BrowserWindow, dialog, ipcMain, Menu, session } = require('electron');

     4	const { registerIpc } = require('./ipc');

     9	const CSP = [
     10	  "default-src 'self'",
     11	  "script-src 'self'",
     12	  "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'",
     13	  "img-src 'self' data:",
     14	  "font-src 'self'",

     25	function sendToRenderer(channel, payload) {
     26	  if (mainWindow && !mainWindow.isDestroyed()) mainWindow.webContents.send(channel, payload);
     27	}
     28	
     29	async function createMainWindow() {
     30	  const preferences = await preferencesStore.load();
     31	  const bounds = preferences.windowBounds || {};
     32	  mainWindow = new BrowserWindow({
     33	    width: 1280,
     34	    height: 820,
     35	    minWidth: 960,
     36	    minHeight: 620,
     37	    ...bounds,
     38	    show: false,
     39	    title: 'Tmux Desk',
     40	    titleBarStyle: 'hiddenInset',
     41	    trafficLightPosition: { x: 15, y: 14 },
     42	    backgroundColor: '#f6f7f9',
     43	    webPreferences: {
     44	      preload: path.join(__dirname, '..', 'preload.js'),
     45	      contextIsolation: true,
     46	      nodeIntegration: false,
     47	      sandbox: true,
     48	    },
     49	  });
     50	  mainWindow.once('ready-to-show', () => mainWindow?.show());
     51	  mainWindow.on('close', () => {
     52	    if (!mainWindow?.isDestroyed()) {
     53	      preferencesStore.save({ windowBounds: mainWindow.getBounds() }).catch(() => {});
     54	    }
     55	  });
     56	  mainWindow.on('closed', () => { mainWindow = null; terminalManager.dispose(); });
     57	  await mainWindow.loadFile(path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'renderer-dist', 'index.html'));
     58	}

     60	function installContentSecurityPolicy() {

     62	    callback({ responseHeaders: { ...details.responseHeaders, 'Content-Security-Policy': [CSP] } });
     63	  });

     70	function installApplicationMenu() {

     72	    {
     73	      label: app.name,
     74	      submenu: [
     75	        { role: 'about' },

     112	
     113	async function bootstrap() {

     5	    .toLocaleLowerCase();

     8	function byRecentActivity(left, right) {
     9	  const activityDifference = Number(right.activity || 0) - Number(left.activity || 0);
     10	  if (activityDifference) return activityDifference;
     11	  return String(left.name || '').localeCompare(String(right.name || ''), 'zh-CN');

     14	function filterAndGroupSessions(sessions, favorites, query, favoritesOnly) {
     15	  const items = Array.isArray(sessions) ? sessions : [];
     16	  const favoriteNames = Array.isArray(favorites) ? favorites : [];
     17	  const favoriteOrder = new Map(favoriteNames.map((name, index) => [name, index]));
     18	  const normalizedQuery = typeof query === 'string' ? query.trim().toLocaleLowerCase() : '';
     19	  const filtered = items.filter((session) => (
     20	    !normalizedQuery || searchableText(session).includes(normalizedQuery)
     21	  ));
     22	  const favoriteSessions = filtered
     23	    .filter((session) => favoriteOrder.has(session.name))
     24	    .sort((left, right) => favoriteOrder.get(left.name) - favoriteOrder.get(right.name));
     25	  const ordinarySessions = favoritesOnly
     26	    ? []
     27	    : filtered.filter((session) => !favoriteOrder.has(session.name)).sort(byRecentActivity);
     28	  return { favorites: favoriteSessions, sessions: ordinarySessions };
     29	}

     31	module.exports = { filterAndGroupSessions };

     const { contextBridge, ipcRenderer } = require('electron');
     
     function on(channel, listener) {
       const wrapped = (_event, payload) => listener(payload);
       ipcRenderer.on(channel, wrapped);
       return () => ipcRenderer.removeListener(channel, wrapped);
     }
     
     contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld('tmuxDesk', Object.freeze({
       getSystemStatus: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('system:get-status'),

       resizeTerminal: (payload) => ipcRenderer.invoke('terminal:resize', payload),
       detachTerminal: () => ipcRenderer.invoke('terminal:detach'),

     === terminal-view ===
     const { Terminal } = require('@xterm/xterm');
     const { FitAddon } = require('@xterm/addon-fit');
     
     const TERMINAL_THEMES = {
       light: {
         background: '#fbfbfc', foreground: '#20242c', cursor: '#1769e0', cursorAccent: '#ffffff',
         selectionBackground: '#b9d4ff99', black: '#20242c', red: '#c9343f', green: '#177245',
         yellow: '#8a6200', blue: '#1769e0', magenta: '#8750ad', cyan: '#087f8c', white: '#e8ebef',
         brightBlack: '#6f7785', brightRed: '#d84b56', brightGreen: '#268657', brightYellow: '#a77700',
         brightBlue: '#3281ee', brightMagenta: '#9b67be', brightCyan: '#1594a1', brightWhite: '#ffffff',
       },
       dark: {
         background: '#111318', foreground: '#d8dde7', cursor: '#8bb8ff', cursorAccent: '#111318',
         selectionBackground: '#31588a99', black: '#20242d', red: '#f06a74', green: '#68d391',
         yellow: '#e7b75d', blue: '#74a9f5', magenta: '#c495e8', cyan: '#67c6d2', white: '#d8dde7',
         brightBlack: '#727b8c', brightRed: '#ff7c86', brightGreen: '#7ae3a3', brightYellow: '#f4c76f',
         brightBlue: '#8bb8ff', brightMagenta: '#d5a8f3', brightCyan: '#7dd8e3', brightWhite: '#ffffff',
       },
     };
     
     class TerminalView {
       constructor({ container, api, onExit, onError }) {
         this.api = api;
         this.onExit = onExit;
         this.onError = onError;
         this.terminalId = null;
         this.attachRequest = 0;
         this.pendingData = new Map();
         this.terminal = new Terminal({
           allowProposedApi: false,
           cursorBlink: true,
           cursorStyle: 'block',
           convertEol: false,
           fontFamily: 'SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, monospace',
           fontSize: 13,
           lineHeight: 1.22,
           letterSpacing: 0,
           scrollback: 10000,
           theme: TERMINAL_THEMES.light,
         });
         this.fitAddon = new FitAddon();
         this.terminal.loadAddon(this.fitAddon);
         this.terminal.open(container);
         this.dataDisposable = this.terminal.onData((data) => {
           if (this.terminalId === null) return;
           this.api.writeTerminal({ terminalId: this.terminalId, data }).catch((error) => this.onError?.(error));
         });
         this.unsubscribeData = api.onTerminalData((payload) => this.handleData(payload));
         this.unsubscribeExit = api.onTerminalExit((payload) => this.handleExit(payload));
         this.resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => this.scheduleFit());
         this.resizeObserver.observe(container);
       }
     
       async attach(sessionId) {
         const request = ++this.attachRequest;
         this.terminalId = null;
         this.pendingData.clear();
         this.terminal.reset();
         this.fitAddon.fit();
         const result = await this.api.attachTerminal({
           sessionId,
           cols: Math.max(2, this.terminal.cols),
           rows: Math.max(1, this.terminal.rows),
         });
         if (request !== this.attachRequest) return null;
         this.terminalId = result.terminalId;
         const pending = this.pendingData.get(this.terminalId);
         if (pending) this.terminal.write(pending);
         this.pendingData.clear();
         this.terminal.focus();
         this.scheduleFit();
         return result;
       }
     
       handleData({ terminalId, data } = {}) {
         if (terminalId === this.terminalId) {
           this.terminal.write(data);
           return;
         }
         if (this.terminalId === null && Number.isInteger(terminalId) && typeof data === 'string') {
           const buffered = `${this.pendingData.get(terminalId) || ''}${data}`;
           this.pendingData.set(terminalId, buffered.slice(-1024 * 1024));
         }
       }
     
       handleExit(payload = {}) {
         if (payload.terminalId !== this.terminalId) return;
         this.terminalId = null;
         this.onExit?.(payload);
       }
     
       setTheme(theme) {
         this.terminal.options.theme = TERMINAL_THEMES[theme] || TERMINAL_THEMES.light;
       }
     
       scheduleFit() {
         cancelAnimationFrame(this.fitFrame);
         this.fitFrame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
           try {
             this.fitAddon.fit();
             if (this.terminalId !== null) {
               this.api.resizeTerminal({
                 terminalId: this.terminalId,
                 cols: Math.max(2, this.terminal.cols),
                 rows: Math.max(1, this.terminal.rows),
               }).catch((error) => this.onError?.(error));
             }
           } catch {}
         });
       }
     
       focus() { this.terminal.focus(); }
     
       async detach() {
         this.attachRequest += 1;
         this.terminalId = null;
         this.pendingData.clear();
         await this.api.detachTerminal();
       }
     
       dispose() {
         cancelAnimationFrame(this.fitFrame);
         this.resizeObserver.disconnect();
         this.dataDisposable.dispose();
         this.unsubscribeData?.();
         this.unsubscribeExit?.();
         this.terminal.dispose();
       }
     }
     
     module.exports = { TerminalView };

⏺ **Root cause reproduced.** When launched from Finder/Dock, the app inherits no `LANG`/`LC_*`, so the tmux CLI runs in the **C locale** and sanitizes its formatted output — replacing the TAB field separators (and every non-ASCII byte) with `_`. So `parseRecords()` splits on `\t`, finds none, and each session record collapses into one garbage field: `name` is `undefined` (empty name), `windows`/`paneCount` become 0, and the `id` no longer matches `$\d+`. That produces exactly the screenshot: blank names, "0 窗口 · 0 面板", plus `会话名称无效` (from `savePreferences(lastSession=undefined)`) and `tmux 目标无效` (from `getSessionDetail`/`attach` with the garbage id).

           2 + const DEFAULT_UTF8_LOCALE = 'en_US.UTF-8';
           3 + 
           4 + // When Tmux Desk is launched from Finder/Dock, the process inherits no
           5 + // LANG/LC_* variables, so the tmux CLI falls back to the C locale and
           6 + // sanitizes its formatted output — replacing the tab field separators (and
           7 + // every non-ASCII byte) with "_". That collapses each `list-sessions` record
           8 + // into one garbage field: blank names, 0 windows, and ids that no longer match
           9 + // `$<n>`. Guaranteeing a UTF-8 locale keeps tmux emitting real tabs + UTF-8.
          10 + function withUtf8Locale(env = process.env) {
          11 +   const source = env || {};
          12 +   if (UTF8_PATTERN.test(source.LC_ALL || source.LC_CTYPE || source.LANG || '')) {
          13 +     return { ...source };
          14 +   }
          15 +   return { ...source, LANG: source.LANG || DEFAULT_UTF8_LOCALE, LC_ALL: DEFAULT_UTF8_LOCALE };
          16 + }
          17 + 
          18 + module.exports = { withUtf8Locale, DEFAULT_UTF8_LOCALE };
          19 +

         193       return { id, name: validName };
         194     }
         195   
         196 +   // Fork a session the way `git checkout -b` forks a branch: a fresh session
         197 +   // that mirrors the source's windows (name + working directory), so you can
         198 +   // diverge from the current setup without disturbing the original.
         199 +   async duplicateSession(sourceTarget, name) {
         200 +     validateTarget(sourceTarget, SESSION_TARGET, '会话');
         201 +     const validName = validateName(name);
         202 +     const { windows, panes } = await this.getSessionDetail(sourceTarget);
         203 +     const ordered = [...windows].sort((left, right) => left.index - right.index);
         204 +     const cwdForWindow = (windowInfo) => {
         205 +       const windowPanes = panes.filter((pane) => pane.windowId === windowInfo.id);
         206 +       const chosen = windowPanes.find((pane) => pane.active) || windowPanes[0];
         207 +       return this.safeDir(chosen && chosen.path);
         208 +     };
         209 + 
         210 +     const first = ordered[0];
         211 +     const firstCwd = first ? cwdForWindow(first) : null;
         212 +     const { stdout } = await this.run([
         213 +       'new-session', '-d', '-P', '-F', '#{session_id}', '-s', validName,
         214 +       ...(firstCwd ? ['-c', firstCwd] : []),
         215 +     ]);
         216 +     const id = String(stdout || '').trim();
         217 + 
         218 +     if (first && first.name) {
         219 +       try { await this.run(['rename-window', '-t', id, first.name]); } catch { /* keep default name */ }
         220 +     }
         221 +     for (const windowInfo of ordered.slice(1)) {
         222 +       const cwd = cwdForWindow(windowInfo);
         223 +       await this.run([
         224 +         'new-window', '-t', id,
         225 +         ...(cwd ? ['-c', cwd] : []),
         226 +         ...(windowInfo.name ? ['-n', windowInfo.name] : []),
         227 +       ]);
         228 +     }
         229 +     return { id, name: validName };
         230 +   }
         231 + 
         232     async renameSession(sessionTarget, name) {
         233       validateTarget(sessionTarget, SESSION_TARGET, '会话');
         234       await this.run(['rename-session', '-t', sessionTarget, validateName(name)]);

⏺ Now the main-process file service (testable listing logic):

```
⏺ Write(src/main/file-service.js)
  ⎿  Wrote 39 lines to src/main/file-service.js
           1 + const fs = require('node:fs/promises');
           2 + const path = require('node:path');
           3 + 
           4 + function validatePath(target) {
           5 +   if (typeof target !== 'string' || !target || target.length > 4096 || target.includes('\0') || !path.isAbsolute(target)) {
           6 +     throw new Error('路径无效');
           7 +   }
           8 +   return target;
           9 + }
          10 + 
          11 + // A read-only directory listing used by the Finder-style side panel. Folders
          12 + // sort before files; symlinks are resolved so a linked directory still expands.
          13 + async function listDirectory(dirPath, { fsImpl = fs } = {}) {
          14 +   const dir = validatePath(dirPath);
          15 +   const dirents = await fsImpl.readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
          16 +   const entries = await Promise.all(dirents.map(async (dirent) => {
          17 +     const full = path.join(dir, dirent.name);
          18 +     let isDir = dirent.isDirectory();
          19 +     const isSymlink = dirent.isSymbolicLink();
          20 +     let size = 0;
          21 +     try {
          22 +       const stat = isSymlink ? await fsImpl.stat(full) : await fsImpl.lstat(full);
          23 +       isDir = isSymlink ? stat.isDirectory() : isDir;
          24 +       size = stat.isFile() ? stat.size : 0;
          25 +     } catch {
          26 +       // Broken symlink or permission denied — surface the entry, just without a size.
          27 +     }
          28 +     return { name: dirent.name, path: full, isDir, isSymlink, hidden: dirent.name.startsWith('.'), size };
          29 +   }));
          30 +   entries.sort((left, right) => (
          31 +     left.isDir === right.isDir
          32 +       ? left.name.localeCompare(right.name, 'zh-CN', { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' })
          33 +       : left.isDir ? -1 : 1
          34 +   ));
          35 +   return { path: dir, entries };
          36 + }
          37 + 
          38 + module.exports = { listDirectory, validatePath };
          39 +

````
⏺ Write(vendor/README.md)
  ⎿  Wrote 30 lines to vendor/README.md
           1 + # vendor/bin — 可选的自带 tmux
           2 + 
           3 + Tmux Desk 是 tmux 的图形前端，运行时**依赖本机的 tmux**（它通过命令行驱动 tmux，
           4 + 并用 node-pty attach 到会话）。默认情况下应用按以下顺序查找 tmux：
           5 + 
           6 + 1. `Contents/Resources/bin/tmux`（本目录打包进 `.app` 后的位置）—— **如果存在就优先用它**
           7 + 2. `/opt/homebrew/bin/tmux`、`/usr/local/bin/tmux`、`/usr/bin/tmux`
           8 + 3. `which tmux`（PATH）
           9 + 
          10 + 所以对于**没装 tmux 的用户**，只要把一个可执行的 tmux 二进制放到 `vendor/bin/tmux`，
          11 + 再打包（`npm run package:mac`），应用就会自带 tmux、开箱即用，无需用户 `brew install tmux`。
          12 + 
          13 + ## 放置一个自带 tmux
          14 + 
          15 + 把二进制放到 `vendor/bin/tmux` 并确保可执行：
          16 + 
          17 + ```bash
          18 + cp /path/to/tmux vendor/bin/tmux
          19 + chmod +x vendor/bin/tmux
          20 + ```
          21 + 
          22 + 注意：
          23 + - 需要与目标架构匹配（arm64 包放 arm64 的 tmux，x64 包放 x64 的）。
          24 + - **建议使用静态链接的 tmux**（自带 libevent / ncurses），否则运行时会缺 dylib。
          25 +   Homebrew 的 tmux 依赖 `/opt/homebrew` 下的 dylib，直接拷进来在别人机器上会跑不起来。
          26 +   可用 `dylibbundler` 把依赖一起搬进来并改写 rpath，或自行编译一个静态版本。
          27 + - 打包配置见根 `package.json` 的 `build.extraResources`（本目录会被复制为 `Resources/bin`）。
          28 + 
          29 + `vendor/bin/.gitkeep` 只是为了让空目录存在、让打包配置始终有效；不放二进制时应用会自动回退到本机 tmux。
          30 + 
````

    const sessions = await api.listSessions();
    const src = sessions[0];
    const dup = await api.duplicateSession({ source: src.id, name: 'zzz-probe-copy' });
    console.log('PROBE duplicated=' + JSON.stringify(dup));
    const after = await api.listSessions();
    const made = after.find(s => s.name === 'zzz-probe-copy');
    console.log('PROBE copyWindows=' + (made ? made.windows : 'MISSING') + ' srcWindows=' + src.windows);
    await api.killSession(dup.id);
    console.log('PROBE cleanupKilled=' + dup.id);
  } catch (e) { console.log('PROBE ERROR ' + (e && e.message)); }
}, 3500);
EOF

⏺ All clean, 44 tests pass. Let me update the README to document the new features and the fix:

````
⏺ Read(README.md)
  ⎿  1	# Tmux Desk
     2	
     3	Tmux Desk 是一个纯本地的 macOS tmux 桌面管理器。它采用类似 Codex 的工作台布局，把本机 tmux 会话、窗口、pane 和真实终端集中到一个桌面窗口中。
     4	
     5	## 功能
     6	
     7	- 查看、搜索、创建、连接、收藏、重命名和关闭本机 tmux 会话
     8	- 顶部标签切换 tmux window，面板菜单快速定位 pane
     9	- 基于 xterm.js + node-pty 的完整交互终端，支持颜色、鼠标和全屏终端程序
     10	- `⌘K` 命令面板与常用 macOS 快捷键
     11	- 亮色、暗色和跟随系统主题；首次启动默认亮色
     12	- 外部 Terminal 中创建或关闭会话后自动刷新
     13	- 不含登录、联网、遥测、云同步或插件
     14	
     15	## 环境要求
     16	
     17	- macOS（首版支持 Apple Silicon，工程保留 x64/universal 打包脚本）
     18	- Node.js 22+
     19	- Xcode Command Line Tools
     20	- tmux：
     21	
     22	```bash
     23	brew install tmux
     24	```
     25	
     26	应用会依次检测 `/opt/homebrew/bin/tmux`、`/usr/local/bin/tmux`、`/usr/bin/tmux` 和 PATH。
     27	
     28	## 本地运行
     29	
     30	```bash
     31	cd /Users/liwei/Documents/tools/terminal-cli
     32	npm install
     33	npm start
     34	```
     35	
     36	`npm install` 会使用 Electron Builder 为当前 Electron 版本重建 `node-pty` 原生模块。
     37	
     38	## 测试
     39	
     40	```bash
     41	npm test
     42	npm run test:integration
     43	npm run build
     44	```
     45	
     46	集成测试使用独立的 `tmux -L tmux-desk-test-*` socket，不会连接、修改或关闭用户现有 tmux 会话。
     47	
     48	## 打包
     49	
     50	```bash
     51	# Apple Silicon DMG + zip
     52	CSC_IDENTITY_AUTO_DISCOVERY=false npm run package:mac
     53	
     54	# Intel
     55	CSC_IDENTITY_AUTO_DISCOVERY=false npm run package:mac:x64
     56	
     57	# Universal
     58	CSC_IDENTITY_AUTO_DISCOVERY=false npm run package:mac:universal
     59	```
     60	
     61	输出位于 `dist/`。正式发布时请通过 Electron Builder 环境变量配置 Developer ID 签名和 Apple 公证，不要将证书或凭据写入仓库。
     62	
     63	## 快捷键
     64	
     65	| 快捷键 | 操作 |
     66	|---|---|
     67	| `⌘K` | 打开命令面板 |
     68	| `⌘N` | 新建会话 |
     69	| `⌘F` | 搜索会话 |
     70	| `⌘R` | 刷新会话 |
     71	| `⌘⇧T` | 在亮色、暗色、跟随系统之间切换 |
     72	| `⌘1…9` | 切换当前会话的 tmux window |
     73	| `Esc` | 关闭菜单或对话框 |
     74	
     75	## 本地数据与隐私
     76	
     77	终端输入、输出和 tmux 会话数据不会被记录或发送。主题、收藏、上次会话和窗口尺寸保存在：
     78	
     79	```text
     80	~/Library/Application Support/Tmux Desk/preferences.json
     81	```
     82	
     83	应用的渲染进程没有 Node.js 权限，只能通过白名单 IPC 调用 tmux 与终端操作；控制命令使用 `execFile` 参数数组，不拼接 shell 命令。
     84	
     85	## 常见问题
     86	
     87	### 应用提示未找到 tmux
     88	
     89	运行 `brew install tmux` 后重启应用或点击“重新检测”。Apple Silicon Homebrew 默认路径是 `/opt/homebrew/bin/tmux`。
     90	
     91	### node-pty ABI 错误
     92	
     93	依赖可能是由另一 Node/Electron 版本构建的，重新运行：
     94	
     95	```bash
     96	npm run postinstall
     97	```
     98	
     99	### 切换会话会不会杀掉任务？
     100	
     101	不会。切换时只关闭当前应用窗口对应的 `tmux attach` 客户端，tmux server 和会话内进程继续运行。只有明确选择“关闭会话”并二次确认后，才会执行 `tmux kill-session`。
     102	
     103	### App Store 计划
     104	
     105	当前版本面向本地 DMG/zip 分发。Mac App Store 强制 App Sandbox，而沙盒会限制外部 tmux 进程和用户既有 tmux socket 的访问。未来 MAS 版本需要独立验证 entitlements、tmux 分发方式和 socket 策略，不能保证与非沙盒版本能力完全一致。
     106
````

⏺ All four requests are done and verified end-to-end against your real tmux server (I ran the app under a Finder-like stripped environment to reproduce and confirm the fix). Here's the summary:

## 1. "tmux 目标无效" 修复 ✅ (根因找到)

**根因**：从**访达/程序坞启动**时，App 不继承 shell 的 `LANG`/`LC_*`，tmux 子进程退回 **C locale**，会把 `list-sessions` 输出里的**制表符分隔符和中文/emoji 全替换成 `_`**。于是 `parseRecords()` 按 `\t` 分割拿到一整段乱码：会话名变空、窗口数=0、id 不再匹配 `$<n>` → 触发 `会话名称无效`（`savePreferences(lastSession=undefined)`）和 `tmux 目标无效`（`getSessionDetail`/`attach`）。完全对应你截图里的每一个症状。

**修复**：新增 `src/main/locale-env.js`，给 tmux 的 `execFile` 和 node-pty attach 进程强制注入 UTF-8 locale（缺失才注入，已有 UTF-8 则保留）。已加回归测试并实机验证：现在会话名全部正确显示。

## 2. 是否依赖 tmux / 能否预装 ✅

是的，它是 tmux 的图形前端（命令行驱动 + node-pty attach）。已加**自带 tmux 支持**：查找顺序改为 **`.app` 内 `Resources/bin/tmux` → Homebrew → 系统 → PATH**，并配好 electron-builder `extraResources`。只要把二进制放到 `vendor/bin/tmux` 再打包即可开箱即用（`vendor/README.md` 有说明，含静态链接注意事项；默认为空则回退本机 tmux）。另外把"未找到 tmux"空状态加了**复制安装命令**按钮。

## 3. 复制会话（类似 checkout -b）✅

新增 `duplicateSession`：按源会话的**窗口名 + 各窗口目录**派生一个新会话，原会话不动。入口在会话右键菜单「复制会话…」和命令面板，弹窗预填 `<名字>-copy`（自动避重名）。实机验证：复制 `$0` 生成新会话、窗口数一致、并清理无残留。

## 4. 右侧 Finder 式文件浏览器 ✅

可收起/展开的右侧面板（`⌘B` 或标题栏图标），根目录跟随当前会话的活动 pane 目录：懒加载文件树、文件夹优先排序、隐藏文件淡显；右键菜单 **打开（默认 App）/ 打开方式…（选其他 App，走 `open -a`）/ 在访达中显示 / 复制路径**。全部 IPC 路径实机验证通过（`listFiles` 返回 16 项、排序正确）。

## 顺带：左上角活动栏不协调 ✅

`>_` 品牌标与下方几乎一样的终端导航按钮直接堆叠、且离红绿灯太挤。已把品牌标略缩小、加了一条分隔线，视觉上变成"品牌标 → 分隔 → 导航组"，节奏更整齐（见上方截图）。

⏺ Bash(cd /Users/liwei/Documents/tools
git commit -m "$(cat <<'EOF'
fix(tmux-desk): force UTF-8 locale + add duplicate-session, file browser, bundled-tmux

Fix the "tmux 目标无效 / 会话名称无效" failure when the app is launched from
Finder/Dock: with no inherited LANG/LC_*, tmux falls back to the C locale and
replaces its tab field separators (and CJK/emoji names) with "_", so every
session record collapses into one garbage field. Inject a guaranteed UTF-8
locale into both the tmux execFile env and the node-pty attach env.

Also:
- Duplicate/copy session (like `git checkout -b`): fork a new session mirroring
  the source's windows (name + cwd). Menu + command-palette + modal.
- Finder-style collapsible right file browser (⌘B): lazy tree rooted at the
  active session dir; open with default app / open-with / reveal / copy path.
- Bundled-tmux support: resolveTmuxPath prefers Resources/bin/tmux; vendor/bin
  + extraResources plumbing; actionable missing-tmux empty state.
- Tidy the activity-rail top (brand mark → divider → nav group).

Adds locale-env / file-service / duplicate-session regression tests (44 pass).

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 <noreply@anthropic.com>

⏺ Now I understand the mechanics. Two fixes:

**Shift+Enter**: In a terminal, Enter and Shift+Enter both send `\r` — Claude Code can't tell them apart, so it submits. Claude Code treats **meta+Enter (`\x1b\r`)** as "insert newline" (that's what Option+Enter does). I'll map Shift+Enter to that sequence.

**IME caret**: xterm only sets the hidden textarea's position (not its font metrics) during composition, so the OS anchors the candidate window using the textarea's default caret height. Forcing the textarea to match the cell metrics aligns it.

```
⏺ Read(src/renderer/terminal-view.js [offset=36, limit=30])

     37	      letterSpacing: 0,
     38	      scrollback: 10000,
     39	      theme: TERMINAL_THEMES.light,
     40	    });

     42	    this.terminal.loadAddon(this.fitAddon);
     43	    this.terminal.open(container);
     44	    this.dataDisposable = this.terminal.onData((data) => {
     45	      if (this.terminalId === null) return;
     46	      this.api.writeTerminal({ terminalId: this.terminalId, data }).catch((error) => this.onError?.(error));
     47	    });
     48	    this.unsubscribeData = api.onTerminalData((payload) => this.handleData(payload));

     50	    this.resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => this.scheduleFit());

     63	      rows: Math.max(1, this.terminal.rows),
     64	    });
     65	    if (request !== this.attachRequest) return null;

```
⏺ Write(src/renderer/session-api-doc.js)
  ⎿  Wrote 59 lines to src/renderer/session-api-doc.js
           1 + // The copyable "how to get sessions into Tmux Desk" guide, written for an LLM
           2 + // agent (Claude Code etc.). Tmux Desk just reads the default tmux server, so the
           3 + // whole "API" is a handful of plain tmux commands — documented here as Markdown.
           4 + function sessionApiMarkdown({ tmuxPath = 'tmux' } = {}) {
           5 +   const detected = tmuxPath || 'tmux';
           6 +   return `# 把会话导入 Tmux Desk（给 AI Agent 的指南）
           7 + 
           8 + Tmux Desk 直接读取本机**默认 tmux 服务器**上的会话。你（AI agent）只要在本机用 tmux
           9 + 建好会话，Tmux Desk 会在约 2 秒内自动显示（或点右上角刷新）。没有额外的 API / 端口 / 鉴权。
          10 + 
          11 + ## 快速开始：在当前项目目录建一个会话
          12 + 
          13 + 在你的工作目录里执行：
          14 + 
          15 + \`\`\`bash
          16 + tmux new-session -d -s "my-project" -c "$(pwd)"
          17 + \`\`\`
          18 + 
          19 + - \`-d\` 后台创建（不 attach）；在 Tmux Desk 里点击该会话即可连接。
          20 + - \`-s\` 会话名；\`-c\` 工作目录，**必须是绝对路径**。
          21 + 
          22 + ## 会话命名规则
          23 + 
          24 + - 1–80 个字符，首尾空格会被去除。
          25 + - **不能包含** \`.\`（点）、\`:\`（冒号）或控制字符（否则在 App 内重命名会被拒绝）。
          26 + - 可以包含中文、emoji、\`/\`、空格，例如 \`channel-biz (DMS渠道)\`。
          27 + 
          28 + ## 加窗口 / 设置启动命令
          29 + 
          30 + \`\`\`bash
          31 + # 在会话里再开一个命名窗口，并指定它的工作目录
          32 + tmux new-window -t "my-project" -c "$(pwd)/subdir" -n "server"
          33 + 
          34 + # 在会话里执行一条启动命令（先按字面输入文本，再单独发一个回车）
          35 + tmux send-keys -t "my-project" -l -- "npm run dev"
          36 + tmux send-keys -t "my-project" Enter
          37 + \`\`\`
          38 + 
          39 + ## 基于当前工作目录一键建/复用会话（幂等）
          40 + 
          41 + 把这段直接丢给 shell 执行即可：
          42 + 
          43 + \`\`\`bash
          44 + name="$(basename "$(pwd)")"            # 用当前目录名作为会话名
          45 + tmux has-session -t "$name" 2>/dev/null \\
          46 +   || tmux new-session -d -s "$name" -c "$(pwd)"
          47 + echo "会话 \\"$name\\" 已就绪，打开 Tmux Desk 即可连接。"
          48 + \`\`\`
          49 + 
          50 + ## 说明
          51 + 
          52 + - Tmux Desk 使用**默认 tmux socket**（等同于直接敲 \`tmux\`），与你在终端里的 \`tmux\` 是同一台服务器。
          53 + - 本机检测到的 tmux 可执行文件：\`${detected}\`
          54 + - 关闭 Tmux Desk 窗口只会断开 attach，**不会杀掉会话**；只有在 App 里明确「关闭会话」才会 \`kill-session\`。
          55 + `;
          56 + }
          57 + 
          58 + module.exports = { sessionApiMarkdown };
          59 + 
```

cp /tmp/main.js.bak src/main/main.js
cp /tmp/app.js.bak renderer-dist/app.js
grep 'PROBE' /tmp/reveal.log)
