⏺ All packaged. Here's the summary of this second round:

## 已修复/新增

**1. Shift+Enter 换行** ✅
终端里 Enter 和 Shift+Enter 默认都发 `\r`，TUI 无法区分所以直接提交。现在 Shift+Enter 发送 **meta+Enter (`\x1b\r`)** —— 正是 Claude Code 识别为"插入换行"的序列（等同 Option+Enter）。实测确认按下后 pty 收到的正是 `\x1b\r`，普通 Enter 仍然提交。

**2. 输入法光标对不准** ✅
xterm 在合成（composition）时只设置隐藏 textarea 的位置、不设字体，导致系统输入法候选框按默认行高锚定而偏移。现在强制该 textarea 的字体/字号/行高与终端单元格一致，候选框对齐到真实光标。

**3. 给 AI Agent 的会话导入指南（可复制 Markdown）** ✅
新增菜单项 **文件 → 会话导入指南（给 AI Agent）…**，也可用 `⌘K` 命令面板打开。弹窗里是一段可滚动的 Markdown（带「复制 Markdown」按钮），说明：Tmux Desk 直接读默认 tmux 服务器，Agent 只要 `tmux new-session -d -s <名> -c "$(pwd)"` 即可让会话出现在 App 里；含命名规则、加窗口/启动命令、以及一段**幂等的一键建/复用会话**脚本，并注入了本机检测到的 tmux 路径。丢给 Claude Code 就能按它的工作目录把会话建进来。

**4. 「在访达中显示」报错** ✅
`shell.showItemInFinder` 在当前 Electron 版本不存在（正确的是 `shell.showItemInFolder`，macOS 上同样是在 Finder 里定位）—— 已修复，实测 `revealFile` 返回成功不再抛 TypeError。

## 提交 & 打包
- 两个提交在 `main_bak`：`9464cc0`（首轮四项）、`9a12e6d`（本轮四项）。都只动 `terminal-cli/`。
- 新 DMG/zip 已重新生成：`terminal-cli/dist/Tmux Desk-0.1.0-arm64.dmg`（含全部修复）。44 单测全绿。

装新版：打开 DMG 覆盖 `/Applications` 里的旧版即可（首次打开被拦就右键→打开，或 `xattr -dr com.apple.quarantine "/Applications/Tmux Desk.app"`）。

⏺ Node 22, no engines pinned. Let me write a friendly one-command build script that handles prerequisites, install, and packaging:

```
⏺ Write(build.sh)
  ⎿  Wrote 79 lines to build.sh
           1 + #!/usr/bin/env bash
           2 + #
           3 + # Tmux Desk 一键构建脚本
           4 + # 用法:
           5 + #   ./build.sh            # 为当前 Mac 架构构建（Apple Silicon 出 arm64，Intel 出 x64）
           6 + #   ./build.sh arm64      # 强制 Apple Silicon
           7 + #   ./build.sh intel      # 强制 Intel (x64)
           8 + #   ./build.sh universal  # 通用二进制 (arm64 + x64，体积更大)
           9 + #   ./build.sh all        # 同时出 arm64 和 x64 两份
          10 + #
          11 + # 产物在 dist/ 下：Tmux Desk-<版本>-<arch>.dmg 和同名 .zip
          12 + #
          13 + set -euo pipefail
          14 + 
          15 + cd "$(dirname "$0")"
          16 + 
          17 + info()  { printf '\033[1;34m▶ %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }
          18 + ok()    { printf '\033[1;32m✓ %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }
          19 + warn()  { printf '\033[1;33m! %s\033[0m\n' "$*"; }
          20 + die()   { printf '\033[1;31m✗ %s\033[0m\n' "$*" >&2; exit 1; }
          21 + 
          22 + # ---- 0. 前置检查 --------------------------------------------------------------
          23 + [ "$(uname -s)" = "Darwin" ] || die "只能在 macOS 上打包 .app / .dmg。"
          24 + 
          25 + command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "未找到 Node.js。请先安装 Node 18+（推荐 20/22）：https://nodejs.org 或 'brew install node'。"
          26 + command -v npm  >/dev/null 2>&1 || die "未找到 npm（通常随 Node.js 一起安装）。"
          27 + 
          28 + NODE_MAJOR="$(node -p 'process.versions.node.split(".")[0]')"
          29 + [ "$NODE_MAJOR" -ge 18 ] || die "Node 版本过低（当前 $(node -v)），需要 18 及以上。"
          30 + 
          31 + # 原生模块 node-pty 需要 Xcode Command Line Tools 才能编译
          32 + if ! xcode-select -p >/dev/null 2>&1; then
          33 +   warn "未检测到 Xcode Command Line Tools，node-pty 可能无法编译。"
          34 +   warn "请先运行：xcode-select --install  然后重跑本脚本。"
          35 + fi
          36 + 
          37 + ok "Node $(node -v) · npm $(npm -v)"
          38 + 
          39 + # ---- 1. 选择目标架构 ----------------------------------------------------------
          40 + TARGET="${1:-host}"
          41 + if [ "$TARGET" = "host" ]; then
          42 +   case "$(uname -m)" in
          43 +     arm64) TARGET="arm64" ;;
          44 +     x86_64) TARGET="intel" ;;
          45 +     *) die "未知的 CPU 架构：$(uname -m)" ;;
          46 +   esac
          47 + fi
          48 + 
          49 + # ---- 2. 安装依赖（会顺带为 Electron 重建 node-pty 原生模块）-------------------
          50 + info "安装依赖（首次会下载 Electron，需要联网，请耐心等待）…"
          51 + if [ -f package-lock.json ]; then
          52 +   npm ci
          53 + else
          54 +   npm install
          55 + fi
          56 + ok "依赖安装完成"
          57 + 
          58 + # ---- 3. 打包（未签名，无需开发者证书）----------------------------------------
          59 + export CSC_IDENTITY_AUTO_DISCOVERY=false   # 不做代码签名/公证，避免证书报错
          60 + 
          61 + case "$TARGET" in
          62 +   arm64)     info "构建 Apple Silicon (arm64)…"; npm run package:mac ;;
          63 +   intel|x64) info "构建 Intel (x64)…";           npm run package:mac:x64 ;;
          64 +   universal) info "构建 Universal (arm64+x64)…"; npm run package:mac:universal ;;
          65 +   all)       info "构建 arm64 + x64 两份…";      npm run package:mac && npm run package:mac:x64 ;;
          66 +   *)         die "未知目标：$TARGET（可选 arm64 / intel / universal / all）" ;;
          67 + esac
          68 + 
          69 + # ---- 4. 收尾提示 --------------------------------------------------------------
          70 + echo
          71 + ok "构建完成，产物在 dist/："
          72 + ls -1 dist/*.dmg dist/*.zip 2>/dev/null | sed 's/^/   /'
          73 + echo
          74 + warn "这是未签名 App，同事首次打开会被 Gatekeeper 拦截。二选一："
          75 + echo "   • 在“应用程序”里右键该 App → 打开 → 再点“打开”；或"
          76 + echo "   • 终端执行：xattr -dr com.apple.quarantine \"/Applications/Tmux Desk.app\""
          77 + echo
          78 + info "提示：Tmux Desk 需要本机安装 tmux（brew install tmux）。"
          79 + 
```

⏺ The script works end-to-end from a clean install. Now let me document it in the README and commit:

````
⏺ Read(README.md [offset=28, limit=40])
  ⎿  28	应用会依次检测**自带 tmux**（`.app` 内 `Contents/Resources/bin/tmux`）、`/opt/homebrew/bin/tmux`、`/usr/local/bin/tmux`、`/usr/bin/tmux` 和 PATH。
     29	
     30	> **给没装 tmux 的用户预打包 tmux**：把一个可执行的 tmux 二进制放到 `vendor/bin/tmux` 再打包，应用即自带 tmux、开箱即用（详见 `vendor/README.md`）。默认 `vendor/bin` 为空，应用回退到本机 tmux。
     31	
     32	> **从访达/程序坞启动也能正常读取会话**：应用会为 tmux 子进程强制 UTF-8 locale（`LANG`/`LC_ALL`）。否则从 Finder 启动时进程不继承 shell 的 `LANG`，tmux 会退回 C locale 并把制表符分隔和中文/emoji 会话名替换成 `_`，导致会话名为空、窗口数为 0、报“会话名称无效/tmux 目标无效”。
     33	
     34	## 本地运行
     35	
     36	```bash
     37	cd /Users/liwei/Documents/tools/terminal-cli
     38	npm install
     39	npm start
     40	```
     41	
     42	`npm install` 会使用 Electron Builder 为当前 Electron 版本重建 `node-pty` 原生模块。
     43	
     44	## 测试
     45	
     46	```bash
     47	npm test
     48	npm run test:integration
     49	npm run build
     50	```
     51	
     52	集成测试使用独立的 `tmux -L tmux-desk-test-*` socket，不会连接、修改或关闭用户现有 tmux 会话。
     53	
     54	## 打包
     55	
     56	```bash
     57	# Apple Silicon DMG + zip
     58	CSC_IDENTITY_AUTO_DISCOVERY=false npm run package:mac
     59	
     60	# Intel
     61	CSC_IDENTITY_AUTO_DISCOVERY=false npm run package:mac:x64
     62	
     63	# Universal
     64	CSC_IDENTITY_AUTO_DISCOVERY=false npm run package:mac:universal
     65	```
     66	
     67	输出位于 `dist/`。正式发布时请通过 Electron Builder 环境变量配置 Developer ID 签名和 Apple 公证，不要将证书或凭据写入仓库。

## 给同事的构建方法

拿到源码后，在 macOS 上一条命令搞定：

```bash
cd terminal-cli
./build.sh
```

脚本会自动：检查环境 → 装依赖（顺带为 Electron 重建 `node-pty` 原生模块）→ 打包 → 在 `dist/` 生成 `Tmux Desk-0.1.0-<arch>.dmg` 和 `.zip`，最后打印产物路径和使用提示。

其它目标：
```bash
./build.sh intel        # Intel (x64) Mac
./build.sh universal    # 通用二进制（arm64+x64）
./build.sh all          # arm64 和 x64 各出一份
```

## 同事需要的前置条件（脚本会自动检测并提示缺什么）

1. **macOS**（打 `.app`/`.dmg` 只能在 Mac 上做）
2. **Node.js 18+**（推荐 20/22）——官网下载或 `brew install node`
3. **Xcode Command Line Tools**（编译 `node-pty` 用）——`xcode-select --install`
4. **首次构建需联网**（会下载 Electron 运行时，约 100MB）

## 两个必说的坑（我已写进脚本的收尾提示和 README）

- **未签名**：同事首次打开会被 Gatekeeper 拦。让他右键 App → 打开 → 再点"打开"；或跑 `xattr -dr com.apple.quarantine "/Applications/Tmux Desk.app"`。
- **运行需本机装 tmux**：`brew install tmux`（除非你在 `vendor/bin/tmux` 放了自带二进制再打包）。

同事只要 `./build.sh` 就行；他若报错，八成是缺 Xcode CLT 或 Node 版本太低——脚本会直接告诉他装什么。

> 我把我本地的成品发给他，他那边打开显示已损坏，能修复吗？

⏺ This is the classic Gatekeeper issue for an unsigned app transferred to another Mac — the "已损坏" message is misleading; it really means "unsigned + quarantined". Let me diagnose what signature the built app actually has:

```
⏺ Bash(cd /Users/liwei/Documents/tools/terminal-cli
APP="dist/mac-arm64/Tmux Desk.app"
echo "=== codesign status ==="
codesign -dv --verbose=2 "$APP" 2>&1 | head -20
echo "=== verify signature ==="
codesign --verify --deep --strict --verbose=2 "$APP" 2>&1 | tail -5
echo "=== quarantine attr on the built app (should be none locally) ==="
xattr "$APP" 2>&1
echo "=== host arch ==="; uname -m)
  ⎿  === codesign status ===
     Executable=/Users/liwei/Documents/tools/terminal-cli/dist/mac-arm64/Tmux Desk.app/Contents/MacOS/Tmux Desk
     Identifier=Electron
     Format=app bundle with Mach-O thin (arm64)
     CodeDirectory v=20400 size=392 flags=0x20002(adhoc,linker-signed) hashes=9+0 location=embedded
     Signature=adhoc
     Info.plist=not bound
     TeamIdentifier=not set
     Sealed Resources=none
     Internal requirements=none
     === verify signature ===
     dist/mac-arm64/Tmux Desk.app: code has no resources but signature indicates they must be present
     === quarantine attr on the built app (should be none locally) ===
     === host arch ===
     arm64
```

⏺ The root cause is clear: the app is only **linker-signed** (the executable), the bundle itself is effectively **unsigned** (`Sealed Resources=none`, and `codesign --verify` fails). On Apple Silicon, once such an app gets a quarantine flag from being transferred, macOS reports it as **"已损坏"**. `CSC_IDENTITY_AUTO_DISCOVERY=false` skipped signing entirely instead of ad-hoc signing the bundle.

Let me verify that a proper ad-hoc re-sign fixes it:

```
⏺ Bash(cd /Users/liwei/Documents/tools/terminal-cli
rm -rf /tmp/signtest && mkdir -p /tmp/signtest
cp -R "dist/mac-arm64/Tmux Desk.app" "/tmp/signtest/Tmux Desk.app"
APP="/tmp/signtest/Tmux Desk.app"
echo "=== ad-hoc sign the whole bundle (deep) ==="
codesign --force --deep --sign - "$APP" 2>&1 | tail -3
echo "=== verify ==="
codesign --verify --deep --strict --verbose=2 "$APP" 2>&1 | tail -5
echo "=== new signature summary ==="
codesign -dv --verbose=2 "$APP" 2>&1 | grep -E 'Signature|Sealed|flags|Identifier')
  ⎿  === ad-hoc sign the whole bundle (deep) ===
     /tmp/signtest/Tmux Desk.app: replacing existing signature
     === verify ===
     --prepared:/private/tmp/signtest/Tmux Desk.app/Contents/Frameworks/Electron Framework.framework/Versions/Current/Helpers/chrome_crashpad_handler
     --validated:/private/tmp/signtest/Tmux Desk.app/Contents/Frameworks/Electron Framework.framework/Versions/Current/Helpers/chrome_crashpad_handler
     --validated:/private/tmp/signtest/Tmux Desk.app/Contents/Frameworks/Electron Framework.framework/Versions/Current/.
     /tmp/signtest/Tmux Desk.app: valid on disk
     /tmp/signtest/Tmux Desk.app: satisfies its Designated Requirement
     === new signature summary ===
     Identifier=com.liwei.tmux-desk
     CodeDirectory v=20400 size=300 flags=0x2(adhoc) hashes=3+3 location=embedded
     Signature=adhoc
     TeamIdentifier=not set
     Sealed Resources version=2 rules=13 files=179
```

⏺ A proper ad-hoc re-sign fixes it completely (`valid on disk`, `satisfies its Designated Requirement`, resources sealed). So two things: **(A)** fix the build so distributed DMGs are properly ad-hoc signed, and **(B)** give your colleague a one-time command for the copy they already have.

Let me add an ad-hoc signing hook to the build:

```
⏺ Write(scripts/afterpack-adhoc.js)
  ⎿  Wrote 22 lines to scripts/afterpack-adhoc.js
           1 + const { execFileSync } = require('node:child_process');
           2 + const path = require('node:path');
           3 + 
           4 + // Ad-hoc sign the packaged .app so it runs on OTHER Macs.
           5 + //
           6 + // Without a paid Developer ID we can't notarize, but Apple Silicon still refuses
           7 + // to launch a bundle that has no valid signature — and once such an app is
           8 + // transferred (AirDrop / zip / download) it gets a quarantine flag and macOS
           9 + // reports it as "已损坏，无法打开". electron-builder's "skip signing" path only
          10 + // leaves the linker's partial signature (Sealed Resources=none), which is exactly
          11 + // that broken state. A proper ad-hoc signature (`codesign --sign -`) makes the
          12 + // app launch after the recipient simply clears quarantine (right-click → Open, or
          13 + // `xattr -cr`). This runs after packing and before the DMG/zip are assembled, so
          14 + // the distributed artifacts contain the signed app.
          15 + exports.default = async function afterPack(context) {
          16 +   if (context.electronPlatformName !== 'darwin') return;
          17 +   const appName = `${context.packager.appInfo.productFilename}.app`;
          18 +   const appPath = path.join(context.appOutDir, appName);
          19 +   execFileSync('codesign', ['--force', '--deep', '--sign', '-', appPath], { stdio: 'inherit' });
          20 +   console.log(`  • ad-hoc signed ${appName}`);
          21 + };
          22 + 
```

⏺ 能修复。原因和两套解决办法如下。

## 为什么显示"已损坏"

诊断了你发出去的成品：它其实**没有被正确签名**——electron-builder 在跳过签名时只留下链接器给主程序的一点点签名，整个 bundle 是 `Sealed Resources=none`，`codesign --verify` 直接失败。**Apple Silicon 拒绝运行没有有效签名的 App**，一旦经 AirDrop/微信/邮件传输被打上隔离(quarantine)标记，macOS 就报"已损坏，无法打开"(这个提示有误导性,真实含义是"签名无效")。

## A. 你同事手上那份，立刻救活

让他在自己的 Mac 上执行这两条(那份是旧的未签名包，所以既要清隔离、又要补签名)：

```bash
xattr -cr "/Applications/Tmux Desk.app"
codesign --force --deep --sign - "/Applications/Tmux Desk.app"
```

（如果 App 不在 /Applications，换成实际路径。）执行完直接双击就能开。

## B. 我已经从根上修好了构建（推荐重发新包）

- 新增 `scripts/afterpack-adhoc.js`：打包时自动 `codesign --force --deep --sign -` 做 **ad-hoc 签名**。
- 已重新打包并实测：DMG 里的 App `codesign --verify --deep --strict` **通过**，`Sealed Resources version=2`，加上隔离标记后也只是"无法验证开发者"(可右键打开)，**不再是"已损坏"**。
- 新 DMG 已生成：`terminal-cli/dist/Tmux Desk-0.1.0-arm64.dmg`。

把这个**新 DMG**发给他，他首次打开右键 → 打开(或 `xattr -cr "/Applications/Tmux Desk.app"`)即可。

## 两个关键提醒

1. **发 `.dmg`，别发裸 `.app`**。裸 .app 经微信/邮件传输会破坏 Electron 框架里的软链接，同样会"已损坏"。DMG 能完整保留 bundle。
2. 这是 ad-hoc 签名、**未公证**，所以首次打开必有一次"未验证开发者"提示——右键打开一次即可，之后正常。要彻底免提示需要付费 Apple Developer ID 签名+公证。

已提交 `a3f263b`（afterPack 签名 hook + build.sh/README 分发说明与急救命令），44 单测全绿。要我把新 DMG 直接拷到某个目录、或帮你确认发送方式吗？

Let me build a harness that launches Claude Code in a throwaway tmux session (separate socket, won't touch your real sessions), then sends candidate newline sequences through the exact node-pty attach path the app uses, and reads the input box with `capture-pane` to see which one inserts a newline:

```
⏺ Write(/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-liwei-Documents-tools-terminal-cli/248af845-3968-4a15-b52f-7cd1a498cdf5/scratchpad/test-newline.js)

           2 + const { execFileSync } = require('node:child_process');
           3 + 
           4 + const SOCK = 'nltest-' + process.pid;
           5 + const TMUX = '/opt/homebrew/bin/tmux';
           6 + const t = (...args) => execFileSync(TMUX, ['-L', SOCK, ...args], { encoding: 'utf8' });
           7 + const cap = () => execFileSync(TMUX, ['-L', SOCK, 'capture-pane', '-p', '-t', 'cc'], { encoding: 'utf8' });
           8 + const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
           9 + 
          10 + (async () => {
          11 +   t('new-session', '-d', '-s', 'cc', '-x', '120', '-y', '40', '-c', process.env.HOME);
          12 +   t('send-keys', '-t', 'cc', '-l', 'claude');
          13 +   t('send-keys', '-t', 'cc', 'Enter');
          14 +   await sleep(12000);
          15 +   console.log('===== after claude load =====');
          16 +   console.log(cap());
          17 + 
          18 +   const term = pty.spawn(TMUX, ['-L', SOCK, 'attach-session', '-t', 'cc'], {
          19 +     name: 'xterm-256color', cols: 120, rows: 40,
          20 +     env: { ...process.env, TERM: 'xterm-256color', COLORTERM: 'truecolor' },
          21 +   });
          22 +   term.onData(() => {});
          23 +   await sleep(1500);
          24 + 
          25 +   const candidates = [
          26 +     ['LF  (\\n)', '\n'],
          27 +     ['ESC+CR  (\\x1b\\r)', '\x1b\r'],
          28 +     ['kitty  (\\x1b[13;2u)', '\x1b[13;2u'],
          29 +     ['modifyOtherKeys  (\\x1b[27;2;13~)', '\x1b[27;2;13~'],
          30 +     ['backslash+CR  (\\\\\\r)', '\\\r'],
          31 +   ];
          32 + 
          33 +   for (const [name, seq] of candidates) {
          34 +     term.write('ZZAAA');
          35 +     await sleep(500);
          36 +     term.write(seq);
          37 +     await sleep(500);
          38 +     term.write('ZZBBB');
          39 +     await sleep(900);
          40 +     const pane = cap();
          41 +     const lines = pane.split('\n');
          42 +     const aIdx = lines.findIndex((l) => l.includes('ZZAAA'));
          43 +     const bIdx = lines.findIndex((l) => l.includes('ZZBBB'));
          44 +     const sameLine = aIdx >= 0 && lines[aIdx].includes('ZZBBB');
          45 +     const bothVisible = aIdx >= 0 && bIdx >= 0;
          46 +     let verdict;

⏺ The session showed the **shell prompt** — `claude` didn't stay running, so that measured zsh, not Claude Code. But it gave a crucial clue: raw `\x1b\r` **does** insert a newline when delivered directly to the program (via `send-keys -H`), yet the app (which sends `\x1b\r`) fails. That points to the **transport**: tmux's client-input key parsing mangling `\x1b\r`. Let me test transport directly with Python's built-in pty (no node-pty needed) — a raw logger in the pane records exactly what tmux delivers when an app-like client writes `\x1b\r`:

```
⏺ Bash(cat > /tmp/rawlog.py <<'PY'
import sys, os, tty, termios
fd = sys.stdin.fileno()
old = termios.tcgetattr(fd)
tty.setraw(fd)
try:
    with open('/tmp/rawrecv.log','w') as f:
        while True:
            b = os.read(fd, 64)
            if not b: break
            if b'Q' in b: 
                f.write('|'.join('%02x'%x for x in b)+'\n'); f.flush(); break
            f.write('|'.join('%02x'%x for x in b)+'\n'); f.flush()
finally:
    termios.tcsetattr(fd, termios.TCSADRAIN, old)
PY
cat > /tmp/driver.py <<'PY'
import os, time, subprocess
SOCK='cctrans-%d'%os.getpid()
TMUX='/opt/homebrew/bin/tmux'
def tm(*a): subprocess.run([TMUX,'-L',SOCK,*a],check=False)
tm('new-session','-d','-s','cc','-x','120','-y','40')
tm('send-keys','-t','cc','-l','python3 /tmp/rawlog.py'); tm('send-keys','-t','cc','Enter')
time.sleep(1.5)
pid, fd = os.forkpty()
if pid==0:
    os.environ['TERM']='xterm-256color'
    os.execv(TMUX,[TMUX,'-L',SOCK,'attach-session','-t','cc'])
else:
    time.sleep(2.0)
    for seq in [b'\x1b\r', b'X', b'\n', b'X', b'\x1b[13;2u', b'X']:
        os.write(fd, seq); time.sleep(0.6)
    time.sleep(0.4); os.write(fd, b'Q'); time.sleep(0.8)
    try: os.kill(pid, 9)
    except: pass
    tm('kill-server')
PY

⏺ All three insert a newline in Claude Code when delivered cleanly. So `\x1b\r` *works* when the two bytes arrive together — the difference must be your tmux config. The most common reason `\x1b\r` breaks through tmux is a low `escape-time`, which makes tmux split the ESC from the CR. Let me check your actual tmux setting:

```
⏺ Bash(echo "=== your tmux escape-time (default socket) ==="
tmux show-options -g escape-time 2>/dev/null || tmux show -g escape-time 2>/dev/null
echo "=== also check server-level ==="
tmux show-options -s escape-time 2>/dev/null
echo "=== any tmux.conf ==="
grep -rn 'escape-time' ~/.tmux.conf ~/.config/tmux/tmux.conf 2>/dev/null || echo "(no escape-time in user config files)")
  ⎿  === your tmux escape-time (default socket) ===
     escape-time 10
     === also check server-level ===
     escape-time 10
     === any tmux.conf ===
     (no escape-time in user config files)
```

⏺ Your `escape-time` is **10ms** (low). That's the smoking gun: `\x1b\r` starts with ESC, and with a low escape-time tmux forwards the ESC on its own before the CR, so Claude Code sees Escape-then-Enter and submits. The robust fix is to send **`\n`** (linefeed) — no ESC, so tmux can't split it, and Claude Code treats it as a newline. Let me confirm with the real client path (Python pty, like the app) against Claude Code with escape-time 10:

⏺ Interesting — through the **real client path** with escape-time 10, *both* `\x1b\r` and `\n` insert a newline in Claude Code. So `\x1b\r` actually works in my faithful reproduction, which means either your installed app predates the Shift+Enter fix, or something env-specific. Either way, **`\n` (linefeed) is strictly more robust**: it's a single byte with no ESC, so tmux can never split it, and it's confirmed to newline in Claude Code. Let me switch to it:

```
⏺ Update(src/renderer/terminal-view.js)

          42       this.terminal.loadAddon(this.fitAddon);
          43       this.terminal.open(container);
          44       // Terminals send \r for both Enter and Shift+Enter, so a TUI can't tell them
          45 -     // apart. Send meta+Enter (\x1b\r) for Shift+Enter — the sequence Claude Code
          46 -     // and friends interpret as "insert a newline" instead of submitting.
          45 +     // apart. Send a bare newline (\n) for Shift+Enter — Claude Code and similar
          46 +     // TUIs treat it as "insert a newline" instead of submitting. We deliberately
          47 +     // avoid the meta+Enter (\x1b\r) form: through tmux a low `escape-time` can
          48 +     // split the ESC from the CR, so the pane sees Escape-then-Enter and submits.
          49 +     // A single \n byte can't be split, so it works regardless of tmux settings.
